    1: import OpenAI from "openai";
    2: import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
    3: 
    4: import type {
    5:   LessonExamMarkingConfidence,
    6:   LessonExamMarkingResult,
    7: } from "@/types/interactiveLesson";
    8: 
    9: type RequestBody = {
   10:   topic: string;
   11:   lessonTitle: string;
   12:   question: string;
   13:   maximumMarks: number;
   14:   modelAnswer: string;
   15:   markScheme?: string[];
   16:   guidance?: string[];
   17:   studentResponse: string;
   18: };
   19: 
   20: type FallbackReason =
   21:   "selected" | "missing_api_key" | "quota" | "rate_limit" | "live_error";
   22: 
   23: function clamp(value: number, minimum: number, maximum: number): number {
   24:   return Math.min(maximum, Math.max(minimum, value));
   25: }
   26: 
   27: function clean(value: unknown): string {
   28:   return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
   29: }
   30: 
   31: function cleanArray(value: unknown): string[] {
   32:   return Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(clean).filter(Boolean) : [];
   33: }
   34: 
   35: function normalise(value: string): string {
   36:   return value
   37:     .trim()
   38:     .toLowerCase()
   39:     .replace(/[ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã¢â€žÂ¢ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‹Å“]/g, "'")
   40:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
   41:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   42: }
   43: 
   44: function isNonAnswer(value: string): boolean {
   45:   const answer = normalise(value);
   46:   const compact = answer.replace(/\s+/g, "");
   47: 
   48:   return (
   49:     !answer ||
   50:     [
   51:       "na",
   52:       "none",
   53:       "noanswer",
   54:       "idk",
   55:       "dontknow",
   56:       "notsure",
   57:       "skip",
   58:       "blank",
   59:       "unknown",
   60:       "noidea",
   61:     ].includes(compact)
   62:   );
   63: }
   64: 
   65: function extractKeywords(values: string[]): string[] {
   66:   const stopWords = new Set([
   67:     "about",
   68:     "after",
   69:     "again",
   70:     "answer",
   71:     "because",
   72:     "computer",
   73:     "correct",
   74:     "describe",
   75:     "explain",
   76:     "from",
   77:     "into",
   78:     "question",
   79:     "should",
   80:     "that",
   81:     "their",
   82:     "these",
   83:     "this",
   84:     "using",
   85:     "with",
   86:   ]);
   87: 
   88:   return Array.from(
   89:     new Set(
   90:       values
   91:         .join(" ")
   92:         .toLowerCase()
   93:         .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
   94:         .split(/\s+/)
   95:         .filter((word) => word.length >= 4 && !stopWords.has(word)),
   96:     ),
   97:   );
   98: }
   99: 
  100: function demoResult(
  101:   body: RequestBody,
  102:   reason: FallbackReason,
  103: ): LessonExamMarkingResult {
  104:   const maximumMarks = Math.max(1, body.maximumMarks);
  105: 
  106:   if (isNonAnswer(body.studentResponse)) {
  107:     return {
  108:       mode: "demo",
  109:       awardedMarks: 0,
  110:       maximumMarks,
  111:       percentage: 0,
  112:       confidence: "high",
  113:       matchedPoints: [],
  114:       missingPoints: body.markScheme?.length
  115:         ? body.markScheme
  116:         : ["No assessable response was provided."],
  117:       feedback:
  118:         "No assessable response was provided. Attempt the question and use the mark allocation to decide how much detail is required.",
  119:       improvedAnswer: body.modelAnswer,
  120:       teacherReviewRequired: false,
  121:       markedAt: new Date(),
  122:     };
  123:   }
  124: 
  125:   const points = body.markScheme?.length
  126:     ? body.markScheme
  127:     : body.modelAnswer
  128:         .split(/[.;]\s*/)
  129:         .map((point) => point.trim())
  130:         .filter(Boolean);
  131: 
  132:   const response = normalise(body.studentResponse);
  133:   const matchedPoints = points.filter((point) => {
  134:     const pointKeywords = extractKeywords([point]);
  135:     if (pointKeywords.length === 0) return false;
  136: 
  137:     const matchedCount = pointKeywords.filter((keyword) =>
  138:       response.includes(keyword),
  139:     ).length;
  140: 
  141:     return matchedCount >= Math.min(2, pointKeywords.length);
  142:   });
  143: 
  144:   const missingPoints = points.filter(
  145:     (point) => !matchedPoints.includes(point),
  146:   );
  147: 
  148:   const modelKeywords = extractKeywords([body.modelAnswer]);
  149:   const matchedModelKeywords = modelKeywords.filter((keyword) =>
  150:     response.includes(keyword),
  151:   );
  152: 
  153:   const relevant = matchedPoints.length > 0 || matchedModelKeywords.length >= 2;
  154: 
  155:   let awardedMarks = 0;
  156: 
  157:   if (relevant) {
  158:     awardedMarks = clamp(matchedPoints.length, 0, maximumMarks);
  159: 
  160:     if (
  161:       awardedMarks < maximumMarks &&
  162:       matchedPoints.length > 0 &&
  163:       matchedModelKeywords.length >= 3 &&
  164:       body.studentResponse.trim().split(/\s+/).length >= maximumMarks * 5
  165:     ) {
  166:       awardedMarks += 1;
  167:     }
  168:   }
  169: 
  170:   awardedMarks = clamp(awardedMarks, 0, maximumMarks);
  171:   const percentage = Math.round((awardedMarks / maximumMarks) * 100);
  172: 
  173:   const fallbackMessage =
  174:     reason === "quota"
  175:       ? "Live AI marking quota was unavailable, so conservative demo marking was used."
  176:       : reason === "rate_limit"
  177:         ? "Live AI marking was rate-limited, so conservative demo marking was used."
  178:         : reason === "missing_api_key"
  179:           ? "No live AI key was available, so conservative demo marking was used."
  180:           : reason === "selected"
  181:             ? "Demo marking mode is enabled."
  182:             : "Live AI marking was unavailable, so conservative demo marking was used.";
  183: 
  184:   return {
  185:     mode: "demo",
  186:     awardedMarks,
  187:     maximumMarks,
  188:     percentage,
  189:     confidence: maximumMarks <= 2 ? "medium" : "low",
  190:     matchedPoints,
  191:     missingPoints,
  192:     feedback: `${fallbackMessage} ${
  193:       awardedMarks === maximumMarks
  194:         ? "The response appears to cover the available marking points."
  195:         : awardedMarks === 0
  196:           ? "The response does not yet match a clear question-specific marking point."
  197:           : "The response includes some relevant knowledge but needs greater precision and development."
  198:     }`,
  199:     improvedAnswer: body.modelAnswer,
  200:     teacherReviewRequired: true,
  201:     markedAt: new Date(),
  202:   };
  203: }
  204: 
  205: function buildPrompt(body: RequestBody): string {
  206:   return `
  207: You are an experienced UK GCSE Computer Science examiner.
  208: 
  209: Mark the response only against the supplied question, maximum marks, mark scheme,
  210: model answer and guidance.
  211: 
  212: Rules:
  213: - Blank answers and placeholders such as n/a, idk, not sure, none and no answer receive 0.
  214: - Never award marks for answer length alone.
  215: - Never invent content not present in the student's response.
  216: - Accept technically valid equivalent wording.
  217: - Never award more than the maximum marks.
  218: - Return JSON only.
  219: 
  220: Topic: ${body.topic}
  221: Lesson: ${body.lessonTitle}
  222: Question: ${body.question}
  223: Maximum marks: ${body.maximumMarks}
  224: Mark scheme: ${JSON.stringify(body.markScheme ?? [])}
  225: Model answer: ${body.modelAnswer}
  226: Guidance: ${JSON.stringify(body.guidance ?? [])}
  227: Student response: ${body.studentResponse}
  228: 
  229: Return:
  230: {
  231:   "awardedMarks": 0,
  232:   "confidence": "high | medium | low",
  233:   "matchedPoints": ["string"],
  234:   "missingPoints": ["string"],
  235:   "feedback": "string",
  236:   "improvedAnswer": "string",
  237:   "teacherReviewRequired": true
  238: }
  239: `.trim();
  240: }
  241: 
  242: function parseLiveResult(
  243:   text: string,
  244:   body: RequestBody,
  245: ): LessonExamMarkingResult {
  246:   if (isNonAnswer(body.studentResponse)) {
  247:     return demoResult(body, "selected");
  248:   }
  249: 
  250:   const parsed = JSON.parse(text) as Record<string, unknown>;
  251:   const maximumMarks = Math.max(1, body.maximumMarks);
  252:   const awardedMarks = clamp(Number(parsed.awardedMarks) || 0, 0, maximumMarks);
  253: 
  254:   const confidenceValue = clean(parsed.confidence);
  255:   const confidence: LessonExamMarkingConfidence =
  256:     confidenceValue === "high" ||
  257:     confidenceValue === "medium" ||
  258:     confidenceValue === "low"
  259:       ? confidenceValue
  260:       : "low";
  261: 
  262:   return {
  263:     mode: "live",
  264:     awardedMarks,
  265:     maximumMarks,
  266:     percentage: Math.round((awardedMarks / maximumMarks) * 100),
  267:     confidence,
  268:     matchedPoints: cleanArray(parsed.matchedPoints),
  269:     missingPoints: cleanArray(parsed.missingPoints),
  270:     feedback:
  271:       clean(parsed.feedback) || "Review the response against the mark scheme.",
  272:     improvedAnswer: clean(parsed.improvedAnswer) || body.modelAnswer,
  273:     teacherReviewRequired:
  274:       parsed.teacherReviewRequired === true ||
  275:       confidence === "low" ||
  276:       maximumMarks >= 6,
  277:     markedAt: new Date(),
  278:   };
  279: }
  280: 
  281: function isQuotaError(error: unknown): boolean {
  282:   if (!error || typeof error !== "object") return false;
  283: 
  284:   const candidate = error as {
  285:     status?: unknown;
  286:     code?: unknown;
  287:     type?: unknown;
  288:     error?: { code?: unknown; type?: unknown };
  289:   };
  290: 
  291:   return (
  292:     candidate.status === 429 &&
  293:     (candidate.code === "insufficient_quota" ||
  294:       candidate.type === "insufficient_quota" ||
  295:       candidate.error?.code === "insufficient_quota" ||
  296:       candidate.error?.type === "insufficient_quota")
  297:   );
  298: }
  299: 
  300: export async function POST(request: Request) {
  301:   try {
  302:     const body = (await request.json()) as RequestBody;
  303: 
  304:     if (
  305:       !body ||
  306:       !clean(body.question) ||
  307:       !clean(body.studentResponse) ||
  308:       !Number.isFinite(body.maximumMarks)
  309:     ) {
  310:       return NextResponse.json(
  311:         { error: "A valid lesson exam response is required." },
  312:         { status: 400 },
  313:       );
  314:     }
  315: 
  316:     if (process.env.AI_MARKING_DEMO_MODE === "true") {
  317:       return NextResponse.json(demoResult(body, "selected"));
  318:     }
  319: 
  320:     const apiKey = process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY;
  321: 
  322:     if (!apiKey) {
  323:       return NextResponse.json(
  324:         {
  325:           error:
  326:             "Live AI marking is not configured. Demo marking is available only when AI_MARKING_DEMO_MODE=true.",
  327:         },
  328:         { status: 503 },
  329:       );
  330:     }
  331: 
  332:     try {
  333:       const client = new OpenAI({ apiKey });
  334:       const completion = await client.chat.completions.create({
  335:         model: process.env.OPENAI_MARKING_MODEL || "gpt-4.1-mini",
  336:         temperature: 0.1,
  337:         response_format: { type: "json_object" },
  338:         messages: [
  339:           {
  340:             role: "system",
  341:             content:
  342:               "You are a careful UK GCSE Computer Science examiner. Return valid JSON only.",
  343:           },
  344:           { role: "user", content: buildPrompt(body) },
  345:         ],
  346:       });
  347: 
  348:       const output = completion.choices[0]?.message.content;
  349: 
  350:       if (!output) {
  351:         throw new Error("The marking service returned no content.");
  352:       }
  353: 
  354:       return NextResponse.json(parseLiveResult(output, body));
  355:     } catch (liveError) {
  356:       console.error(
  357:         "Live lesson marking unavailable:",
  358:         liveError,
  359:       );
  360: 
  361:       const reason: FallbackReason = isQuotaError(liveError)
  362:         ? "quota"
  363:         : (liveError as { status?: unknown })?.status === 429
  364:           ? "rate_limit"
  365:           : "live_error";
  366: 
  367:       return NextResponse.json(
  368:         {
  369:           error:
  370:             reason === "quota"
  371:               ? "Live AI marking is temporarily unavailable because the configured API quota has been exhausted."
  372:               : reason === "rate_limit"
  373:                 ? "Live AI marking is temporarily rate-limited. Please try again shortly."
  374:                 : "Live AI marking is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later.",
  375:         },
  376:         { status: 503 },
  377:       );
  378:     }
  379:   } catch (error) {
  380:     console.error("Lesson marking route error:", error);
  381: 
  382:     return NextResponse.json(
  383:       {
  384:         error:
  385:           error instanceof Error
  386:             ? error.message
  387:             : "The lesson answer could not be marked.",
  388:       },
  389:       { status: 500 },
  390:     );
  391:   }
  392: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: app\api\ai\student-tutor\route.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import OpenAI from "openai";
    2: import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
    3: import type { TutorResponse, TutorStudentContext } from "@/types/studentTutor";
    4: 
    5: type Body = {
    6:   studentId: string;
    7:   message: string;
    8:   history: { role: "student" | "assistant"; content: string }[];
    9:   context: TutorStudentContext;
   10: };
   11: const clean = (v: unknown) => (typeof v === "string" ? v.trim() : "");
   12: function demo(body: Body, warning?: string): TutorResponse {
   13:   const c = body.context;
   14:   const first = c.name.split(" ")[0] || "Student";
   15:   const topic =
   16:     c.priorityTopics[0]?.topic ||
   17:     c.recommendedActions[0]?.topic ||
   18:     "Computer Science";
   19:   const m = body.message.toLowerCase();
   20:   let reply = "";
   21:   if (/^(hi|hello|hey)/.test(m))
   22:     reply = `Hi ${first}. Your combined average is ${c.combinedAverage}% and your predicted grade is ${c.predictedGrade}. ${c.priorityTopics.length ? `Your main priority is ${topic}.` : "You have no high-priority topic currently."} What would you like help with?`;
   23:   else if (/revision plan|study plan|what should i revise/.test(m))
   24:     reply = `Here is a focused plan for ${topic}:
   25: 
   26: 1. Review the core lesson for 10 minutes.
   27: 2. Write three facts from memory.
   28: 3. Complete a short retrieval quiz.
   29: 4. Attempt one exam-style question.
   30: 5. Compare your answer with the mark scheme.
   31: 
   32: Aim for 25-30 minutes.`;
   33:   else if (/predicted grade|what grade/.test(m))
   34:     reply = `Your current platform estimate is grade ${c.currentGrade}, with a predicted grade of ${c.predictedGrade}. This is not an official or guaranteed exam-board prediction.`;
   35:   else
   36:     reply = `Let us work through that, ${first}. Start by telling me what you already know or the exact step that is confusing. Use this structure: definition -> process -> example -> exam wording. Your current priority topic is ${topic}, so I will connect the explanation to it when relevant.`;
   37:   return {
   38:     reply,
   39:     mode: "demo",
   40:     suggestedPrompts: [
   41:       `Explain ${topic} simply`,
   42:       `Quiz me on ${topic}`,
   43:       "Create a 25-minute revision plan",
   44:       "How can I reach the next grade?",
   45:     ],
   46:     recommendations: c.recommendedActions.slice(0, 3).map((a) => ({
   47:       title: a.title,
   48:       description: a.description,
   49:       href: a.href,
   50:       type: a.type,
   51:     })),
   52:     warning,
   53:   };
   54: }
   55: function prompt(body: Body) {
   56:   return `You are CS Master, a supportive UK secondary Computer Science tutor.
   57: Student context:
   58: ${JSON.stringify(body.context, null, 2)}
   59: Recent conversation:
   60: ${JSON.stringify(body.history.slice(-8), null, 2)}
   61: Student message:
   62: ${body.message}
   63: Use clear UK English. Teach through explanation and checking questions. Do not give unexplained answers to assessed work. Never claim predicted grades are official. Keep under 350 words. Return JSON only: {"reply":"string","suggestedPrompts":["string"],"recommendations":[{"title":"string","description":"string","href":"string","type":"lesson|quiz|exam"}]}`;
   64: }
   65: export async function POST(request: Request) {
   66:   try {
   67:     const body = (await request.json()) as Body;
   68:     if (!body?.studentId || !clean(body.message) || !body.context)
   69:       return NextResponse.json(
   70:         { error: "A valid tutor request is required." },
   71:         { status: 400 },
   72:       );
   73:     if (process.env.AI_STUDENT_TUTOR_DEMO_MODE === "true")
   74:       return NextResponse.json(demo(body, "Demo tutor mode is enabled."));
   75:     const key = process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY;
   76:     if (!key) {
   77:       return NextResponse.json(
   78:         {
   79:           error:
   80:             "AI Tutor is temporarily unavailable because live AI is not configured.",
   81:         },
   82:         { status: 503 },
   83:       );
   84:     }
   85:     try {
   86:       const client = new OpenAI({ apiKey: key });
   87:       const completion = await client.chat.completions.create({
   88:         model: process.env.OPENAI_STUDENT_TUTOR_MODEL || "gpt-4.1-mini",
   89:         temperature: 0.3,
   90:         response_format: { type: "json_object" },
   91:         messages: [
   92:           {
   93:             role: "system",
   94:             content:
   95:               "You are a careful UK secondary Computer Science tutor. Return valid JSON only.",
   96:           },
   97:           { role: "user", content: prompt(body) },
   98:         ],
   99:       });
  100:       const text = completion.choices[0]?.message.content;
  101:       if (!text) throw new Error("No tutor response.");
  102:       const p = JSON.parse(text);
  103:       return NextResponse.json({
  104:         reply: clean(p.reply),
  105:         mode: "live",
  106:         suggestedPrompts: Array.isArray(p.suggestedPrompts)
  107:           ? p.suggestedPrompts.slice(0, 4)
  108:           : [],
  109:         recommendations: Array.isArray(p.recommendations)
  110:           ? p.recommendations.slice(0, 3)
  111:           : [],
  112:       });
  113:     } catch (e) {
  114:       console.error("Live tutor unavailable:", e);
  115:       return NextResponse.json(
  116:         {
  117:           error:
  118:             "AI Tutor is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later.",
  119:         },
  120:         { status: 503 },
  121:       );
  122:     }
  123:   } catch (e) {
  124:     console.error("Tutor route error:", e);
  125:     return NextResponse.json(
  126:       {
  127:         error: e instanceof Error ? e.message : "The tutor could not respond.",
  128:       },
  129:       { status: 500 },
  130:     );
  131:   }
  132: }

    1: "use client";

   10: 
   11: export default function TutorPage() {
   12:   const { user } = useAuth();
   13:   const [context, setContext] = useState<TutorStudentContext | null>(null);
   14:   const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
   15:   const [error, setError] = useState("");
   16:   useEffect(() => {
   17:     async function load() {
   18:       if (!user?.uid) {
   19:         setLoading(false);
   20:         return;
   21:       }
   22:       try {
   23:         setContext(await getStudentTutorContext(user.uid));
   24:       } catch (e) {

   27:         );
   28:       } finally {
   29:         setLoading(false);
   30:       }
   31:     }
   32:     void load();
   33:   }, [user?.uid]);

    1: const reviewScheduleRenderTime = new Date().getTime();
    2: 
    3: import type { AdaptiveTopicMastery } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
    4: 
    5: export default function ReviewSchedule({
    6:   topics,
    7: }: {
    8:   topics: AdaptiveTopicMastery[];
    9: }) {
   10:   const upcoming = [...topics]
   11:     .sort(
   12:       (first, second) =>
   13:         first.nextReviewAt.getTime() - second.nextReviewAt.getTime(),
   14:     )
   15:     .slice(0, 6);
   16: 
   17:   return (
   18:     <div className="rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6">
   19:       <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-teal-600">
   20:         Spaced retrieval
   21:       </p>
   22:       <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black text-slate-950">
   23:         Review schedule
   24:       </h2>
   25: 
   26:       <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
   27:         {upcoming.map((topic) => {
   28:           const due = topic.nextReviewAt.getTime() <= reviewScheduleRenderTime;
   29: 
   30:           return (
   31:             <div
   32:               key={topic.id}
   33:               className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 p-4"
   34:             >
   35:               <div>
   36:                 <p className="font-black text-slate-900">{topic.topic}</p>
   37:                 <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
   38:                   Every {topic.reviewIntervalDays} day
   39:                   {topic.reviewIntervalDays === 1 ? "" : "s"}
   40:                 </p>
   41:               </div>
   42: 
   43:               <span
   44:                 className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
   45:                   due ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700" : "bg-teal-100 text-teal-700"
   46:                 }`}
   47:               >
   48:                 {due
   49:                   ? "Due now"
   50:                   : topic.nextReviewAt.toLocaleDateString("en-GB")}
   51:               </span>
   52:             </div>
   53:           );
   54:         })}
   55:       </div>
   56:     </div>
   57:   );
   58: }

    4: function badgeClass(state: AdaptiveTopicMastery["state"]) {
    5:   if (state === "priority") return "bg-red-100 text-red-700";
    6:   if (state === "forgetting-risk") return "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700";
    7:   if (state === "secure" || state === "mastered") {

    9:   }
   10:   if (state === "new") return "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700";

   12: }

FILE: components\tutor\TutorChat.tsx
==============================================================================================================
    1: "use client";
    2: import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
    3: import toast from "react-hot-toast";
    4: import {
    5:   clearTutorConversation,
    6:   createTutorConversation,
    7:   requestTutorResponse,
    8:   saveTutorExchange,
    9: } from "@/services/studentTutorService";
   10: import type {
   11:   TutorMessage,
   12:   TutorResponse,
   13:   TutorStudentContext,
   14: } from "@/types/studentTutor";
   15: 
   16: export default function TutorChat({
   17:   studentId,
   18:   context,
   19: }: {
   20:   studentId: string;
   21:   context: TutorStudentContext;
   22: }) {
   23:   const [conversationId, setConversationId] = useState("");
   24:   const [messages, setMessages] = useState<TutorMessage[]>([]);
   25:   const [last, setLast] = useState<TutorResponse | null>(null);
   26:   const [text, setText] = useState("");
   27:   const [sending, setSending] = useState(false);
   28:   useEffect(() => {
   29:     void createTutorConversation(studentId)
   30:       .then(setConversationId)
   31:       .catch(() => toast.error("Tutor conversation could not start."));
   32:   }, [studentId]);
   33:   const history = useMemo(
   34:     () => messages.map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content })),
   35:     [messages],
   36:   );
   37:   async function send(value: string) {
   38:     const message = value.trim();
   39:     if (!message || !conversationId || sending) return;
   40:     setText("");
   41:     setMessages((x) => [
   42:       ...x,
   43:       {
   44:         id: `s-${Date.now()}`,
   45:         role: "student",
   46:         content: message,
   47:         createdAt: new Date(),
   48:       },
   49:     ]);
   50:     setSending(true);
   51:     try {
   52:       const r = await requestTutorResponse({
   53:         studentId,
   54:         conversationId,
   55:         message,
   56:         history,
   57:         context,
   58:       });
   59:       setMessages((x) => [
   60:         ...x,
   61:         {
   62:           id: `a-${Date.now()}`,
   63:           role: "assistant",
   64:           content: r.reply,
   65:           createdAt: new Date(),
   66:           mode: r.mode,
   67:         },
   68:       ]);
   69:       setLast(r);
   70:       await saveTutorExchange({
   71:         conversationId,
   72:         studentId,
   73:         studentMessage: message,
   74:         tutorResponse: r,
   75:       });
   76:       if (r.warning) toast(r.warning);
   77:     } catch (e) {
   78:       toast.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Tutor failed.");
   79:     } finally {
   80:       setSending(false);
   81:     }
   82:   }
   83:   async function reset() {
   84:     if (!conversationId) return;
   85:     await clearTutorConversation(conversationId, studentId);
   86:     setMessages([]);
   87:     setLast(null);
   88:     toast.success("Conversation cleared.");
   89:   }
   90:   const topic = context.priorityTopics[0]?.topic || "Computer Science";
   91:   return (
   92:     <div className="space-y-5">
   93:       <div className="flex justify-end">
   94:         <button
   95:           onClick={() => void reset()}
   96:           className="rounded-xl border bg-white px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold"
   97:         >
   98:           Clear conversation
   99:         </button>
  100:       </div>
  101:       {messages.length === 0 && (
  102:         <div className="rounded-3xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-6">
  103:           <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase text-blue-600">
  104:             Personal AI tutor
  105:           </p>
  106:           <h2 className="mt-2 text-2xl font-black">
  107:             Hi {context.name.split(" ")[0]}, what are we learning today?
  108:           </h2>
  109:           <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  110:             {[
  111:               `Explain ${topic} simply`,
  112:               `Quiz me on ${topic}`,
  113:               "Create a 25-minute revision plan",
  114:               "How can I reach the next grade?",
  115:             ].map((p) => (
  116:               <button
  117:                 key={p}
  118:                 onClick={() => void send(p)}
  119:                 className="rounded-xl border border-blue-200 bg-white px-4 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-blue-700"
  120:               >
  121:                 {p}
  122:               </button>
  123:             ))}
  124:           </div>
  125:         </div>
  126:       )}
  127:       <div className="min-h-[420px] space-y-4 rounded-3xl bg-slate-100 p-5">
  128:         {messages.map((m) => (
  129:           <div
  130:             key={m.id}
  131:             className={`flex ${m.role === "assistant" ? "justify-start" : "justify-end"}`}
  132:           >
  133:             <div
  134:               className={`max-w-3xl rounded-3xl px-5 py-4 ${m.role === "assistant" ? "bg-white text-slate-800" : "bg-blue-600 text-white"}`}
  135:             >
  136:               <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-7">
  137:                 {m.content}
  138:               </p>
  139:             </div>
  140:           </div>
  141:         ))}
  142:         {sending && (
  143:           <div className="rounded-3xl bg-white px-5 py-4 text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">
  144:             CS Master Tutor is thinking...
  145:           </div>
  146:         )}
  147:       </div>
  148:       {last && last.recommendations.length > 0 && (
  149:         <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
  150:           {last.recommendations.map((r, i) => (
  151:             <a
  152:               key={`${r.href}-${i}`}
  153:               href={r.href}
  154:               className="rounded-2xl border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 p-4"
  155:             >
  156:               <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-blue-600">
  157:                 {r.type}
  158:               </p>
  159:               <p className="mt-1 font-black">{r.title}</p>
  160:               <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">{r.description}</p>
  161:             </a>
  162:           ))}
  163:         </div>
  164:       )}
  165:       <form
  166:         onSubmit={(e) => {
  167:           e.preventDefault();
  168:           void send(text);
  169:         }}
  170:         className="rounded-3xl border bg-white p-4 shadow-lg"
  171:       >
  172:         <textarea
  173:           rows={3}
  174:           value={text}
  175:           onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
  176:           placeholder="Ask about a topic, paste an answer, or request a revision plan..."
  177:           className="w-full resize-none rounded-2xl px-3 py-2 outline-none"
  178:         />
  179:         <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end">
  180:           <button
  181:             disabled={sending || !text.trim()}
  182:             className="rounded-xl bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 font-bold text-white disabled:bg-slate-300"
  183:           >
  184:             {sending ? "Thinking..." : "Send"}
  185:           </button>
  186:         </div>
  187:       </form>
  188:     </div>
  189:   );
  190: }

    1: import {
    2:   collection,

   23: import type {
   24:   AdaptiveEvidence,
   25:   AdaptiveLearningPlan,
   26: } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
   27: 
   28: const safeString = (
   29:   value: unknown,
   30:   fallback = "",
   31: ): string =>
   32:   typeof value === "string" &&
   33:   value.trim()
   34:     ? value.trim()
   35:     : fallback;
   36: 
   37: const safeNumber = (
   38:   value: unknown,
   39: ): number =>
   40:   typeof value === "number" &&
   41:   Number.isFinite(value)
   42:     ? value
   43:     : 0;
   44: 
   45: const average = (
   46:   values: number[],
   47: ): number =>
   48:   values.length
   49:     ? Math.round(
   50:         values.reduce(
   51:           (sum, value) =>
   52:             sum + value,
   53:           0,
   54:         ) / values.length,
   55:       )
   56:     : 0;
   57: 
   58: function groupEvidence(
   59:   evidence: AdaptiveEvidence[],
   60: ) {
   61:   const groups =
   62:     new Map<
   63:       string,
   64:       AdaptiveEvidence[]
   65:     >();
   66: 
   67:   evidence.forEach((item) => {
   68:     const normalised =
   69:       normaliseTopic(item.topic);
   70: 
   71:     const existing =
   72:       groups.get(
   73:         normalised.topicTitle,
   74:       ) || [];
   75: 
   76:     groups.set(
   77:       normalised.topicTitle,
   78:       [
   79:         ...existing,
   80:         {
   81:           ...item,
   82:           topic:
   83:             normalised.topicTitle,
   84:         },
   85:       ],
   86:     );
   87:   });
   88: 
   89:   return Array.from(
   90:     groups.entries(),
   91:   ).map(
   92:     ([topic, items]) => ({
   93:       topic,
   94:       evidence: items,
   95:     }),
   96:   );
   97: }
   98: 
   99: function resolveRawTopic(
  100:   data: Record<string, unknown>,
  101:   fallback = "",
  102: ): string {
  103:   return (
  104:     safeString(
  105:       data.topicTitle,
  106:     ) ||
  107:     safeString(
  108:       data.topic,
  109:     ) ||
  110:     safeString(
  111:       data.curriculumTopic,
  112:     ) ||
  113:     safeString(
  114:       data.quizTitle,
  115:     ) ||
  116:     safeString(
  117:       data.title,
  118:       fallback,
  119:     )
  120:   );
  121: }
  122: 
  123: /*
  124:  * Generic labels describe an assessment type rather than
  125:  * an actual Computer Science curriculum topic.
  126:  *
  127:  * These must never become mastery topics.
  128:  */
  129: function isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  130:   value: string,
  131: ): boolean {
  132:   const normalised = value
  133:     .trim()
  134:     .toLowerCase()
  135:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
  136:     .trim();
  137: 
  138:   return [
  139:     "",
  140:     "quiz",
  141:     "assigned quiz",
  142:     "ai quiz",
  143:     "assessment",
  144:     "assigned assessment",
  145:     "practice quiz",
  146:     "exam",
  147:     "written exam",
  148:   ].includes(normalised);
  149: }
  150: 
  151: /*
  152:  * Assignment titles frequently contain presentational suffixes,
  153:  * for example:
  154:  *
  155:  * "character encoding Demo Quiz"
  156:  * "memory and storage Demo Quiz"
  157:  * "merge sort Demo Quiz"
  158:  *
  159:  * Removing those suffixes gives topicNormalisationService a much
  160:  * cleaner curriculum phrase to resolve.
  161:  */
  162: function cleanAssignmentTitle(
  163:   value: string,
  164: ): string {
  165:   return value
  166:     .trim()
  167:     .replace(
  168:       /\s+(demo\s+)?quiz$/i,
  169:       "",
  170:     )
  171:     .replace(
  172:       /\s+practice\s+quiz$/i,
  173:       "",
  174:     )
  175:     .replace(
  176:       /\s+assessment$/i,
  177:       "",
  178:     )
  179:     .trim();
  180: }
  181: 
  182: type AssignmentTopicMetadata = {
  183:   topic: string;
  184:   title: string;
  185: };
  186: 
  187: async function loadAssignmentTopicMetadata(
  188:   assignmentIds: string[],
  189: ): Promise<
  190:   Map<
  191:     string,
  192:     AssignmentTopicMetadata
  193:   >
  194: > {
  195:   const uniqueIds =
  196:     Array.from(
  197:       new Set(
  198:         assignmentIds
  199:           .map((id) =>
  200:             id.trim(),
  201:           )
  202:           .filter(Boolean),
  203:       ),
  204:     );
  205: 
  206:   const entries =
  207:     await Promise.all(
  208:       uniqueIds.map(
  209:         async (
  210:           assignmentId,
  211:         ) => {
  212:           try {
  213:             const snapshot =
  214:               await getDoc(
  215:                 doc(
  216:                   db,
  217:                   "assignments",
  218:                   assignmentId,
  219:                 ),
  220:               );
  221: 
  222:             if (
  223:               !snapshot.exists()
  224:             ) {
  225:               return [
  226:                 assignmentId,
  227:                 {
  228:                   topic: "",
  229:                   title: "",
  230:                 },
  231:               ] as const;
  232:             }
  233: 
  234:             const data =
  235:               snapshot.data();
  236: 
  237:             return [
  238:               assignmentId,
  239:               {
  240:                 topic:
  241:                   resolveRawTopic(
  242:                     data,
  243:                   ),
  244:                 title:
  245:                   safeString(
  246:                     data.title,
  247:                   ),
  248:               },
  249:             ] as const;
  250:           } catch (
  251:             caughtError
  252:           ) {
  253:             /*
  254:              * A missing/legacy assignment should not prevent the
  255:              * rest of the student's adaptive plan from loading.
  256:              */
  257:             console.warn(
  258:               `Unable to resolve adaptive topic for assignment ${assignmentId}:`,
  259:               caughtError,
  260:             );
  261: 
  262:             return [
  263:               assignmentId,
  264:               {
  265:                 topic: "",
  266:                 title: "",
  267:               },
  268:             ] as const;
  269:           }
  270:         },
  271:       ),
  272:     );
  273: 
  274:   return new Map(entries);
  275: }
  276: 
  277: function resolveAssignedQuizTopic({
  278:   resultData,
  279:   assignmentMetadata,
  280: }: {
  281:   resultData: Record<
  282:     string,
  283:     unknown
  284:   >;
  285: 
  286:   assignmentMetadata:
  287:     AssignmentTopicMetadata | null;
  288: }): string | null {
  289:   /*
  290:    * First preference:
  291:    * explicit curriculum/topic metadata stored directly on
  292:    * the result.
  293:    */
  294:   const resultTopic =
  295:     resolveRawTopic(
  296:       resultData,
  297:     );
  298: 
  299:   if (
  300:     resultTopic &&
  301:     !isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  302:       resultTopic,
  303:     )
  304:   ) {
  305:     return cleanAssignmentTitle(
  306:       resultTopic,
  307:     );
  308:   }
  309: 
  310:   /*
  311:    * Second preference:
  312:    * topic metadata on the original assignment.
  313:    */
  314:   const assignmentTopic =
  315:     assignmentMetadata?.topic ||
  316:     "";
  317: 
  318:   if (
  319:     assignmentTopic &&
  320:     !isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  321:       assignmentTopic,
  322:     )
  323:   ) {
  324:     return cleanAssignmentTitle(
  325:       assignmentTopic,
  326:     );
  327:   }
  328: 
  329:   /*
  330:    * Third preference:
  331:    * derive the curriculum topic from the original assignment
  332:    * title.
  333:    *
  334:    * Example:
  335:    * "character encoding Demo Quiz"
  336:    *          ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬Å“
  337:    * "character encoding"
  338:    *          ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â‚¬Â Ã¢â‚¬Å“
  339:    * topicNormalisationService
  340:    */
  341:   const assignmentTitle =
  342:     cleanAssignmentTitle(
  343:       assignmentMetadata?.title ||
  344:         "",
  345:     );
  346: 
  347:   if (
  348:     assignmentTitle &&
  349:     !isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  350:       assignmentTitle,
  351:     )
  352:   ) {
  353:     return assignmentTitle;
  354:   }
  355: 
  356:   /*
  357:    * Do NOT create fake mastery evidence such as "Assigned Quiz".
  358:    *
  359:    * The result remains stored normally in Firestore and can still
  360:    * contribute to assignment history/markbooks. It simply cannot
  361:    * be attributed to curriculum mastery until a real topic can be
  362:    * identified.
  363:    */
  364:   return null;
  365: }
  366: 
  367: export async function getAdaptiveLearningPlan(
  368:   studentId: string,
  369: ): Promise<AdaptiveLearningPlan> {
  370:   const id =
  371:     studentId.trim();
  372: 
  373:   if (!id) {
  374:     throw new Error(
  375:       "A valid student account is required.",
  376:     );
  377:   }
  378: 
  379:   const [
  380:     profile,
  381:     quizSnapshot,
  382:     assignedQuizSnapshot,
  383:     examAssignments,
  384:     interventions,
  385:   ] = await Promise.all([
  386:     getUserProfile(id),
  387: 
  388:     getDocs(
  389:       query(
  390:         collection(
  391:           db,
  392:           "users",
  393:           id,
  394:           "quizResults",
  395:         ),
  396:         orderBy(
  397:           "createdAt",
  398:           "desc",
  399:         ),
  400:       ),
  401:     ),
  402: 
  403:     getDocs(
  404:       query(
  405:         collection(
  406:           db,
  407:           "assignmentResults",
  408:         ),
  409:         where(
  410:           "studentId",
  411:           "==",
  412:           id,
  413:         ),
  414:       ),
  415:     ),
  416: 
  417:     getStudentExamAssignments(
  418:       id,
  419:     ),
  420: 
  421:     getStudentInterventions(
  422:       id,
  423:     ),
  424:   ]);
  425: 
  426:   if (!profile) {
  427:     throw new Error(
  428:       "The student profile could not be loaded.",
  429:     );
  430:   }
  431: 
  432:   const evidence:
  433:     AdaptiveEvidence[] = [];
  434: 
  435:   /*
  436:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  437:    * NORMAL QUIZ EVIDENCE
  438:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  439:    */
  440:   quizSnapshot.docs.forEach(
  441:     (document) => {
  442:       const data =
  443:         document.data();
  444: 
  445:       const topic =
  446:         resolveRawTopic(
  447:           data,
  448:           "Quiz",
  449:         );
  450: 
  451:       /*
  452:        * Avoid producing a generic "Quiz" mastery topic.
  453:        */
  454:       if (
  455:         isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  456:           topic,
  457:         )
  458:       ) {
  459:         return;
  460:       }
  461: 
  462:       evidence.push({
  463:         id:
  464:           `quiz-${document.id}`,
  465: 
  466:         topic:
  467:           cleanAssignmentTitle(
  468:             topic,
  469:           ),
  470: 
  471:         source: "quiz",
  472: 
  473:         score:
  474:           safeNumber(
  475:             data.scorePercent,
  476:           ),
  477: 
  478:         completedAt:
  479:           data.createdAt instanceof
  480:           Timestamp
  481:             ? data.createdAt.toDate()
  482:             : null,
  483: 
  484:         weight: 1,
  485:       });
  486:     },
  487:   );
  488: 
  489:   /*
  490:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  491:    * ASSIGNED QUIZ EVIDENCE
  492:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  493:    *
  494:    * assignmentResults often contain score/result information but
  495:    * not enough curriculum metadata. Resolve the original assignment
  496:    * so its real topic/title can be used.
  497:    */
  498:   const assignedQuizAssignmentIds =
  499:     assignedQuizSnapshot.docs
  500:       .map((document) =>
  501:         safeString(
  502:           document.data()
  503:             .assignmentId,
  504:         ),
  505:       )
  506:       .filter(Boolean);
  507: 
  508:   const assignmentMetadata =
  509:     await loadAssignmentTopicMetadata(
  510:       assignedQuizAssignmentIds,
  511:     );
  512: 
  513:   assignedQuizSnapshot.docs.forEach(
  514:     (document) => {
  515:       const data =
  516:         document.data();
  517: 
  518:       const assignmentId =
  519:         safeString(
  520:           data.assignmentId,
  521:         );
  522: 
  523:       const topic =
  524:         resolveAssignedQuizTopic({
  525:           resultData: data,
  526: 
  527:           assignmentMetadata:
  528:             assignmentMetadata.get(
  529:               assignmentId,
  530:             ) || null,
  531:         });
  532: 
  533:       if (!topic) {
  534:         console.warn(
  535:           `Assigned quiz result ${document.id} has no resolvable curriculum topic and was excluded from topic mastery.`,
  536:         );
  537: 
  538:         return;
  539:       }
  540: 
  541:       evidence.push({
  542:         id:
  543:           `assigned-quiz-${document.id}`,
  544: 
  545:         topic,
  546: 
  547:         source: "quiz",
  548: 
  549:         score:
  550:           safeNumber(
  551:             data.percentage,
  552:           ),
  553: 
  554:         completedAt:
  555:           data.completedAt instanceof
  556:           Timestamp
  557:             ? data.completedAt.toDate()
  558:             : null,
  559: 
  560:         weight: 1.1,
  561:       });
  562:     },
  563:   );
  564: 
  565:   /*
  566:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  567:    * WRITTEN EXAM EVIDENCE
  568:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  569:    */
  570:   const submissions =
  571:     await Promise.all(
  572:       examAssignments.map(
  573:         (assignment) =>
  574:           getExamSubmission(
  575:             assignment.id,
  576:             id,
  577:           ),
  578:       ),
  579:     );
  580: 
  581:   examAssignments.forEach(
  582:     (
  583:       assignment,
  584:       index,
  585:     ) => {
  586:       const submission =
  587:         submissions[index];
  588: 
  589:       if (
  590:         !submission ||
  591:         submission.status !==
  592:           "marked"
  593:       ) {
  594:         return;
  595:       }
  596: 
  597:       const examTopic =
  598:         assignment
  599:           .questionSetSnapshot
  600:           .topic ||
  601:         assignment
  602:           .questionSetTitle ||
  603:         assignment.title;
  604: 
  605:       if (
  606:         isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  607:           examTopic,
  608:         )
  609:       ) {
  610:         return;
  611:       }
  612: 
  613:       evidence.push({
  614:         id:
  615:           `exam-${assignment.id}`,
  616: 
  617:         topic:
  618:           cleanAssignmentTitle(
  619:             examTopic,
  620:           ),
  621: 
  622:         source: "exam",
  623: 
  624:         score:
  625:           submission.percentage,
  626: 
  627:         completedAt:
  628:           submission.markedAt,
  629: 
  630:         weight: 1.4,
  631:       });
  632:     },
  633:   );
  634: 
  635:   /*
  636:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  637:    * INTERVENTION EVIDENCE
  638:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  639:    */
  640:   interventions.forEach(
  641:     (intervention) => {
  642:       const completedSteps =
  643:         intervention.steps.filter(
  644:           (step) =>
  645:             step.status ===
  646:             "completed",
  647:         ).length;
  648: 
  649:       if (!completedSteps) {
  650:         return;
  651:       }
  652: 
  653:       if (
  654:         isGenericAssessmentTopic(
  655:           intervention.topic,
  656:         )
  657:       ) {
  658:         return;
  659:       }
  660: 
  661:       evidence.push({
  662:         id:
  663:           `intervention-${intervention.id}`,
  664: 
  665:         topic:
  666:           intervention.topic,
  667: 
  668:         source:
  669:           "intervention",
  670: 
  671:         score:
  672:           intervention.currentScore ||
  673:           intervention.baselineScore,
  674: 
  675:         completedAt:
  676:           intervention.updatedAt ||
  677:           intervention.completedAt,
  678: 
  679:         weight: 0.8,
  680:       });
  681:     },
  682:   );
  683: 
  684:   /*
  685:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  686:    * LESSON EVIDENCE
  687:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  688:    */
  689:   profile.completedLessons.forEach(
  690:     (
  691:       lessonId,
  692:       index,
  693:     ) => {
  694:       if (
  695:         !lessonId.trim()
  696:       ) {
  697:         return;
  698:       }
  699: 
  700:       evidence.push({
  701:         id:
  702:           `lesson-${index}-${lessonId}`,
  703: 
  704:         topic: lessonId,
  705: 
  706:         source: "lesson",
  707: 
  708:         score: 60,
  709: 
  710:         completedAt: null,
  711: 
  712:         weight: 0.25,
  713:       });
  714:     },
  715:   );
  716: 
  717:   /*
  718:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  719:    * MASTERY + RECOMMENDATIONS
  720:    * ------------------------------------------------------------
  721:    */
  722:   const topics =
  723:     buildTopicMastery(
  724:       groupEvidence(
  725:         evidence,
  726:       ),
  727:     );
  728: 
  729:   const actions =
  730:     buildAdaptiveActions(
  731:       topics,
  732:     );
  733: 
  734:   const assessedTopics =
  735:     topics.filter(
  736:       (topic) =>
  737:         topic.attempts > 0,
  738:     );
  739: 
  740:   const overallMastery =
  741:     average(
  742:       assessedTopics.map(
  743:         (topic) =>
  744:           topic.masteryScore,
  745:       ),
  746:     );
  747: 
  748:   const confidence =
  749:     average(
  750:       assessedTopics.map(
  751:         (topic) =>
  752:           topic.confidenceScore,
  753:       ),
  754:     );
  755: 
  756:   const examReadiness =
  757:     average(
  758:       assessedTopics.map(
  759:         (topic) =>
  760:           Math.round(
  761:             topic.masteryScore *
  762:               0.75 +
  763:               topic.confidenceScore *
  764:                 0.25,
  765:           ),
  766:       ),
  767:     );
  768: 
  769:   const recentTrend =
  770:     average(
  771:       assessedTopics.map(
  772:         (topic) =>
  773:           topic.trend,
  774:       ),
  775:     );
  776: 
  777:   const predictedScore =
  778:     Math.max(
  779:       0,
  780:       Math.min(
  781:         100,
  782:         examReadiness +
  783:           Math.round(
  784:             recentTrend *
  785:               0.25,
  786:           ),
  787:       ),
  788:     );
  789: 
  790:   return {
  791:     studentId: id,
  792: 
  793:     generatedAt:
  794:       new Date(),
  795: 
  796:     overallMastery,
  797: 
  798:     examReadiness,
  799: 
  800:     confidence,
  801: 
  802:     currentGrade:
  803:       indicativeGradeFromPercentage(
  804:         examReadiness,
  805:         profile?.qualification,
  806:       ),
  807: 
  808:     predictedGrade:
  809:       indicativeGradeFromPercentage(
  810:         predictedScore,
  811:         profile?.qualification,
  812:       ),
  813: 
  814:     dueForReviewCount:
  815:       topics.filter(
  816:         (topic) =>
  817:           topic.nextReviewAt.getTime() <=
  818:           Date.now(),
  819:       ).length,
  820: 
  821:     priorityTopicCount:
  822:       topics.filter(
  823:         (topic) =>
  824:           topic.state ===
  825:             "priority" ||
  826:           topic.state ===
  827:             "forgetting-risk",
  828:       ).length,
  829: 
  830:     secureTopicCount:
  831:       topics.filter(
  832:         (topic) =>
  833:           topic.state ===
  834:             "secure" ||
  835:           topic.state ===
  836:             "mastered",
  837:       ).length,
  838: 
  839:     nextAction:
  840:       actions[0] || null,
  841: 
  842:     actions,
  843: 
  844:     topics,
  845:   };
  846: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\adaptiveMasteryService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import type {
    2:   AdaptiveDifficulty,
    3:   AdaptiveEvidence,
    4:   AdaptiveEvidenceSource,
    5:   AdaptiveTopicMastery,
    6:   AdaptiveTopicState,
    7: } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
    8: 
    9: import {
   10:   calculateDaysSince,
   11:   calculateNextReviewDate,
   12:   calculateReviewInterval,
   13: } from "@/services/spacedRepetitionService";
   14: 
   15: type TopicEvidenceGroup = {
   16:   topic: string;
   17:   evidence: AdaptiveEvidence[];
   18: };
   19: 
   20: const clamp = (
   21:   value: number,
   22: ) =>
   23:   Math.max(
   24:     0,
   25:     Math.min(
   26:       100,
   27:       value,
   28:     ),
   29:   );
   30: 
   31: function weightedAverage(
   32:   evidence: AdaptiveEvidence[],
   33: ): number {
   34:   const scored =
   35:     evidence.filter(
   36:       (item) =>
   37:         typeof item.score ===
   38:         "number",
   39:     );
   40: 
   41:   const totalWeight =
   42:     scored.reduce(
   43:       (sum, item) =>
   44:         sum + item.weight,
   45:       0,
   46:     );
   47: 
   48:   if (
   49:     !scored.length ||
   50:     totalWeight === 0
   51:   ) {
   52:     return 0;
   53:   }
   54: 
   55:   return Math.round(
   56:     scored.reduce(
   57:       (sum, item) =>
   58:         sum +
   59:         (item.score || 0) *
   60:           item.weight,
   61:       0,
   62:     ) /
   63:       totalWeight,
   64:   );
   65: }
   66: 
   67: function scoreSequence(
   68:   evidence: AdaptiveEvidence[],
   69: ): number[] {
   70:   return evidence
   71:     .filter(
   72:       (item) =>
   73:         typeof item.score ===
   74:           "number" &&
   75:         item.completedAt,
   76:     )
   77:     .sort(
   78:       (
   79:         first,
   80:         second,
   81:       ) =>
   82:         (
   83:           first.completedAt?.getTime() ||
   84:           0
   85:         ) -
   86:         (
   87:           second.completedAt?.getTime() ||
   88:           0
   89:         ),
   90:     )
   91:     .map(
   92:       (item) =>
   93:         item.score || 0,
   94:     );
   95: }
   96: 
   97: function calculateTrend(
   98:   evidence: AdaptiveEvidence[],
   99: ): number {
  100:   const scores =
  101:     scoreSequence(
  102:       evidence,
  103:     );
  104: 
  105:   if (
  106:     scores.length < 2
  107:   ) {
  108:     return 0;
  109:   }
  110: 
  111:   const midpoint =
  112:     Math.ceil(
  113:       scores.length / 2,
  114:     );
  115: 
  116:   const average = (
  117:     values: number[],
  118:   ) =>
  119:     values.length
  120:       ? values.reduce(
  121:           (
  122:             sum,
  123:             value,
  124:           ) =>
  125:             sum + value,
  126:           0,
  127:         ) /
  128:         values.length
  129:       : 0;
  130: 
  131:   return Math.round(
  132:     average(
  133:       scores.slice(
  134:         midpoint,
  135:       ),
  136:     ) -
  137:       average(
  138:         scores.slice(
  139:           0,
  140:           midpoint,
  141:         ),
  142:       ),
  143:   );
  144: }
  145: 
  146: function latestEvidence(
  147:   evidence: AdaptiveEvidence[],
  148: ): AdaptiveEvidence | null {
  149:   return (
  150:     [...evidence]
  151:       .filter(
  152:         (item) =>
  153:           item.completedAt,
  154:       )
  155:       .sort(
  156:         (
  157:           first,
  158:           second,
  159:         ) =>
  160:           (
  161:             second.completedAt?.getTime() ||
  162:             0
  163:           ) -
  164:           (
  165:             first.completedAt?.getTime() ||
  166:             0
  167:           ),
  168:       )[0] ||
  169:     null
  170:   );
  171: }
  172: 
  173: function determineState({
  174:   masteryScore,
  175:   attempts,
  176:   daysSincePractice,
  177:   trend,
  178: }: {
  179:   masteryScore: number;
  180:   attempts: number;
  181:   daysSincePractice: number;
  182:   trend: number;
  183: }): AdaptiveTopicState {
  184:   if (
  185:     attempts === 0
  186:   ) {
  187:     return "new";
  188:   }
  189: 
  190:   if (
  191:     masteryScore >= 85 &&
  192:     attempts >= 3 &&
  193:     daysSincePractice <= 21
  194:   ) {
  195:     return "mastered";
  196:   }
  197: 
  198:   if (
  199:     masteryScore >= 70 &&
  200:     daysSincePractice > 14
  201:   ) {
  202:     return "forgetting-risk";
  203:   }
  204: 
  205:   if (
  206:     masteryScore >= 70
  207:   ) {
  208:     return "secure";
  209:   }
  210: 
  211:   if (
  212:     masteryScore < 50 ||
  213:     trend <= -15
  214:   ) {
  215:     return "priority";
  216:   }
  217: 
  218:   return "developing";
  219: }
  220: 
  221: function difficultyFor(
  222:   masteryScore: number,
  223: ): AdaptiveDifficulty {
  224:   if (
  225:     masteryScore < 45
  226:   ) {
  227:     return "foundation";
  228:   }
  229: 
  230:   if (
  231:     masteryScore < 75
  232:   ) {
  233:     return "standard";
  234:   }
  235: 
  236:   return "higher";
  237: }
  238: 
  239: function confidence({
  240:   attempts,
  241:   sources,
  242:   averageScore,
  243: }: {
  244:   attempts: number;
  245:   sources: AdaptiveEvidenceSource[];
  246:   averageScore: number;
  247: }): number {
  248:   return clamp(
  249:     Math.min(
  250:       55,
  251:       attempts * 12,
  252:     ) +
  253:       Math.min(
  254:         25,
  255:         sources.length * 10,
  256:       ) +
  257:       (
  258:         averageScore >= 70
  259:           ? 20
  260:           : averageScore >= 50
  261:             ? 12
  262:             : 6
  263:       ),
  264:   );
  265: }
  266: 
  267: function priority({
  268:   masteryScore,
  269:   daysSincePractice,
  270:   trend,
  271:   attempts,
  272:   state,
  273: }: {
  274:   masteryScore: number;
  275:   daysSincePractice: number;
  276:   trend: number;
  277:   attempts: number;
  278:   state: AdaptiveTopicState;
  279: }): number {
  280:   const stateBoost =
  281:     state === "priority"
  282:       ? 15
  283:       : state ===
  284:           "forgetting-risk"
  285:         ? 12
  286:         : state === "new"
  287:           ? 10
  288:           : 0;
  289: 
  290:   return clamp(
  291:     Math.round(
  292:       (
  293:         100 -
  294:         masteryScore
  295:       ) *
  296:         0.55 +
  297:         Math.min(
  298:           25,
  299:           daysSincePractice *
  300:             1.5,
  301:         ) +
  302:         (
  303:           trend < 0
  304:             ? Math.min(
  305:                 20,
  306:                 Math.abs(
  307:                   trend,
  308:                 ),
  309:               )
  310:             : 0
  311:         ) +
  312:         (
  313:           attempts < 2
  314:             ? 10
  315:             : 0
  316:         ) +
  317:         stateBoost,
  318:     ),
  319:   );
  320: }
  321: 
  322: function reason({
  323:   state,
  324:   masteryScore,
  325:   trend,
  326:   daysSincePractice,
  327: }: {
  328:   state: AdaptiveTopicState;
  329:   masteryScore: number;
  330:   trend: number;
  331:   daysSincePractice: number;
  332: }): string {
  333:   if (
  334:     state === "new"
  335:   ) {
  336:     return "This topic has not yet been assessed, so an initial learning check is needed.";
  337:   }
  338: 
  339:   if (
  340:     state ===
  341:     "forgetting-risk"
  342:   ) {
  343:     return `Mastery is ${masteryScore}%, but the topic has not been practised for ${daysSincePractice} days. Retrieval is due.`;
  344:   }
  345: 
  346:   if (
  347:     state === "priority"
  348:   ) {
  349:     return trend < 0
  350:       ? `Mastery is ${masteryScore}% and recent performance has fallen by ${Math.abs(
  351:           trend,
  352:         )} percentage points.`
  353:       : `Mastery is currently ${masteryScore}%, so this topic needs immediate support.`;
  354:   }
  355: 
  356:   if (
  357:     state === "developing"
  358:   ) {
  359:     return `Mastery is ${masteryScore}%. Further guided practice is needed.`;
  360:   }
  361: 
  362:   if (
  363:     state === "mastered"
  364:   ) {
  365:     return `Mastery is ${masteryScore}% across repeated evidence. Use occasional retrieval to maintain it.`;
  366:   }
  367: 
  368:   return `Mastery is ${masteryScore}%. Continue spaced retrieval to keep the topic secure.`;
  369: }
  370: 
  371: export function buildTopicMastery(
  372:   groups: TopicEvidenceGroup[],
  373: ): AdaptiveTopicMastery[] {
  374:   return groups
  375:     .map(
  376:       (
  377:         group,
  378:         index,
  379:       ) => {
  380:         const attempts =
  381:           group.evidence.filter(
  382:             (item) =>
  383:               typeof item.score ===
  384:               "number",
  385:           ).length;
  386: 
  387:         const averageScore =
  388:           weightedAverage(
  389:             group.evidence,
  390:           );
  391: 
  392:         const trend =
  393:           calculateTrend(
  394:             group.evidence,
  395:           );
  396: 
  397:         const latest =
  398:           latestEvidence(
  399:             group.evidence,
  400:           );
  401: 
  402:         const latestScore =
  403:           typeof latest?.score ===
  404:           "number"
  405:             ? latest.score
  406:             : averageScore;
  407: 
  408:         const lastPractisedAt =
  409:           latest?.completedAt ||
  410:           null;
  411: 
  412:         const daysSincePractice =
  413:           calculateDaysSince(
  414:             lastPractisedAt,
  415:           );
  416: 
  417:         /*
  418:          * After seven days without graded practice, gradually
  419:          * reduce mastery to represent forgetting.
  420:          *
  421:          * The penalty remains capped at 20 percentage points.
  422:          */
  423:         const recencyPenalty =
  424:           Math.min(
  425:             20,
  426:             Math.max(
  427:               0,
  428:               daysSincePractice -
  429:                 7,
  430:             ) * 1.2,
  431:           );
  432: 
  433:         /*
  434:          * Base mastery must use a complete 100% weighting.
  435:          *
  436:          * Historical weighted evidence contributes 75%.
  437:          * The latest result contributes 25%, giving recent
  438:          * evidence extra influence without replacing the
  439:          * student's broader evidence history.
  440:          *
  441:          * Trend and recency are then applied as adjustments.
  442:          *
  443:          * Previous implementation used 70% + 20% = 90%,
  444:          * unintentionally reducing stable scores by 10%.
  445:          * Example: 20% evidence became 18% mastery.
  446:          */
  447:         const masteryScore =
  448:           clamp(
  449:             Math.round(
  450:               averageScore *
  451:                 0.75 +
  452:                 latestScore *
  453:                   0.25 +
  454:                 Math.max(
  455:                   -10,
  456:                   Math.min(
  457:                     10,
  458:                     trend *
  459:                       0.2,
  460:                   ),
  461:                 ) -
  462:                 recencyPenalty,
  463:             ),
  464:           );
  465: 
  466:         const evidenceSources =
  467:           Array.from(
  468:             new Set(
  469:               group.evidence.map(
  470:                 (item) =>
  471:                   item.source,
  472:               ),
  473:             ),
  474:           );
  475: 
  476:         const state =
  477:           determineState({
  478:             masteryScore,
  479:             attempts,
  480:             daysSincePractice,
  481:             trend,
  482:           });
  483: 
  484:         const reviewIntervalDays =
  485:           calculateReviewInterval({
  486:             masteryScore,
  487:             attempts,
  488:             state,
  489:           });
  490: 
  491:         const nextReviewAt =
  492:           calculateNextReviewDate({
  493:             lastPractisedAt,
  494:             intervalDays:
  495:               reviewIntervalDays,
  496:           });
  497: 
  498:         return {
  499:           id:
  500:             `mastery-${index}`,
  501: 
  502:           topic:
  503:             group.topic,
  504: 
  505:           masteryScore,
  506: 
  507:           confidenceScore:
  508:             confidence({
  509:               attempts,
  510:               sources:
  511:                 evidenceSources,
  512:               averageScore,
  513:             }),
  514: 
  515:           priorityScore:
  516:             priority({
  517:               masteryScore,
  518:               daysSincePractice,
  519:               trend,
  520:               attempts,
  521:               state,
  522:             }),
  523: 
  524:           attempts,
  525: 
  526:           averageScore,
  527: 
  528:           latestScore,
  529: 
  530:           trend,
  531: 
  532:           lastPractisedAt,
  533: 
  534:           nextReviewAt,
  535: 
  536:           daysSincePractice,
  537: 
  538:           reviewIntervalDays,
  539: 
  540:           state,
  541: 
  542:           recommendedDifficulty:
  543:             difficultyFor(
  544:               masteryScore,
  545:             ),
  546: 
  547:           evidenceSources,
  548: 
  549:           reason:
  550:             reason({
  551:               state,
  552:               masteryScore,
  553:               trend,
  554:               daysSincePractice,
  555:             }),
  556:         };
  557:       },
  558:     )
  559:     .sort(
  560:       (
  561:         first,
  562:         second,
  563:       ) =>
  564:         second.priorityScore -
  565:         first.priorityScore,
  566:     );
  567: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\adaptiveRecommendationService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { curriculumRegistry } from "@/data/curriculum/curriculumRegistry";
    2: 
    3: import type {
    4:   AdaptiveLearningAction,
    5:   AdaptiveTopicMastery,
    6: } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
    7: 
    8: const encoded = (value: string) =>
    9:   encodeURIComponent(value);
   10: 
   11: function normaliseLookupValue(
   12:   value: string,
   13: ): string {
   14:   return value
   15:     .trim()
   16:     .toLowerCase()
   17:     .replace(/&/g, "and")
   18:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, " ")
   19:     .trim();
   20: }
   21: 
   22: function resolveCurriculumTopicId(
   23:   topic: AdaptiveTopicMastery,
   24: ): string | null {
   25:   const candidates = [
   26:     topic.topic,
   27:     topic.id,
   28:   ]
   29:     .map(normaliseLookupValue)
   30:     .filter(Boolean);
   31: 
   32:   if (candidates.length === 0) {
   33:     return null;
   34:   }
   35: 
   36:   const match =
   37:     curriculumRegistry.find(
   38:       (curriculumTopic) => {
   39:         const searchableValues = [
   40:           curriculumTopic.id,
   41:           curriculumTopic.title,
   42:           ...(curriculumTopic.aliases || []),
   43:         ].map(normaliseLookupValue);
   44: 
   45:         return candidates.some(
   46:           (candidate) =>
   47:             searchableValues.includes(
   48:               candidate,
   49:             ),
   50:         );
   51:       },
   52:     );
   53: 
   54:   return match?.id || null;
   55: }
   56: 
   57: function learningHref(
   58:   topic: AdaptiveTopicMastery,
   59: ): string {
   60:   const curriculumTopicId =
   61:     resolveCurriculumTopicId(topic);
   62: 
   63:   /*
   64:    * Preferred route:
   65:    * resolve the adaptive topic through the curriculum registry.
   66:    *
   67:    * Example:
   68:    * Character Encoding
   69:    *     â†“
   70:    * curriculumRegistry alias/title match
   71:    *     â†“
   72:    * id: "characters"
   73:    *     â†“
   74:    * /learn/characters
   75:    */
   76:   if (curriculumTopicId) {
   77:     return `/learn/${encoded(
   78:       curriculumTopicId,
   79:     )}`;
   80:   }
   81: 
   82:   /*
   83:    * Defensive fallback.
   84:    *
   85:    * If a topic has not yet been registered in the curriculum,
   86:    * return to the searchable Learn catalogue rather than creating
   87:    * a broken /learn/[topicId] route.
   88:    */
   89:   return `/learn?search=${encoded(
   90:     topic.topic,
   91:   )}`;
   92: }
   93: 
   94: export function buildAdaptiveActions(
   95:   topics: AdaptiveTopicMastery[],
   96: ): AdaptiveLearningAction[] {
   97:   const actions: AdaptiveLearningAction[] =
   98:     [];
   99: 
  100:   topics
  101:     .filter(
  102:       (topic) =>
  103:         topic.state !== "mastered",
  104:     )
  105:     .slice(0, 3)
  106:     .forEach((topic, index) => {
  107:       const priority: AdaptiveLearningAction["priority"] =
  108:         topic.priorityScore >= 70
  109:           ? "high"
  110:           : topic.priorityScore >= 45
  111:             ? "medium"
  112:             : "low";
  113: 
  114:       /*
  115:        * New or weak topics should take the learner directly
  116:        * into the corresponding curriculum topic wherever one
  117:        * exists.
  118:        */
  119:       if (
  120:         topic.state === "new" ||
  121:         topic.masteryScore < 45
  122:       ) {
  123:         actions.push({
  124:           id: `lesson-${topic.id}`,
  125:           type: "lesson",
  126:           title: `Review ${topic.topic}`,
  127:           description: topic.reason,
  128:           topic: topic.topic,
  129:           href: learningHref(topic),
  130:           estimatedMinutes: 12,
  131:           xpReward: 25,
  132:           priority,
  133:         });
  134:       }
  135: 
  136:       /*
  137:        * Retrieval practice remains topic-specific.
  138:        */
  139:       actions.push({
  140:         id: `quiz-${topic.id}`,
  141:         type: "quiz",
  142:         title:
  143:           `Retrieval quiz: ${topic.topic}`,
  144:         description:
  145:           `Use ${topic.recommendedDifficulty} difficulty to check recall and update mastery.`,
  146:         topic: topic.topic,
  147:         href:
  148:           `/quiz?topic=${encoded(
  149:             topic.topic,
  150:           )}&difficulty=${encoded(
  151:             topic.recommendedDifficulty,
  152:           )}`,
  153:         estimatedMinutes: 8,
  154:         xpReward: 30,
  155:         priority,
  156:       });
  157: 
  158:       /*
  159:        * Written exam practice remains assignment-driven.
  160:        * The student can only attempt papers that have actually
  161:        * been assigned to them.
  162:        */
  163:       if (
  164:         index === 0 ||
  165:         topic.masteryScore >= 40
  166:       ) {
  167:         actions.push({
  168:           id: `exam-${topic.id}`,
  169:           type: "exam",
  170:           title:
  171:             `Exam practice: ${topic.topic}`,
  172:           description:
  173:             "Apply knowledge in a written response and compare it with the mark scheme.",
  174:           topic: topic.topic,
  175:           href:
  176:             "/assignments?filter=exams",
  177:           estimatedMinutes: 12,
  178:           xpReward: 40,
  179:           priority:
  180:             priority === "high"
  181:               ? "high"
  182:               : "medium",
  183:         });
  184:       }
  185:     });
  186: 
  187:   return actions.slice(0, 6);
  188: }

==============================================================================================================
FILE: services\analytics\interventionReviewCycleService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import {
    2:   doc,
    3:   serverTimestamp,
    4:   updateDoc,
    5: } from "firebase/firestore";
    6: 
    7: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
    8: import type { InterventionImpact } from "@/types/interventionImpact";
    9: import type {
   10:   InterventionReviewCycle,
   11:   InterventionReviewDecision,
   12: } from "@/types/interventionReviewCycle";
   13: 
   14: function changeText(
   15:   label: string,
   16:   value: number | null,
   17:   suffix = "",
   18: ): string | null {
   19:   if (value === null || value === 0) {
   20:     return null;
   21:   }
   22: 
   23:   const direction =
   24:     value > 0 ? "increased" : "decreased";
   25: 
   26:   return `${label} ${direction} by ${Math.abs(value)}${suffix}.`;
   27: }
   28: 
   29: export function buildInterventionReviewCycle(
   30:   impact: InterventionImpact,
   31: ): InterventionReviewCycle {
   32:   const allChanges = [
   33:     changeText("Attainment", impact.attainmentChange, "%"),
   34:     changeText("Completion", impact.completionChange, "%"),
   35:     changeText("Topic mastery", impact.topicMasteryChange, "%"),
   36:     changeText("Working grade", impact.workingGradeChange),
   37:   ].filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value));
   38: 
   39:   const successSignals = allChanges.filter(
   40:     (value) => value.includes("increased"),
   41:   );
   42: 
   43:   const concernSignals = allChanges.filter(
   44:     (value) => value.includes("decreased"),
   45:   );
   46: 
   47:   if (!impact.baseline) {
   48:     return {
   49:       phase: "baseline_needed",
   50:       headline: "Capture a baseline before judging impact",
   51:       explanation:
   52:         "This intervention does not yet have a reliable before-intervention comparison point.",
   53:       evidenceReady: false,
   54:       reassessmentRecommended: false,
   55:       canCloseSuccessful: false,
   56:       recommendedDecision: "continue",
   57:       successSignals,
   58:       concernSignals,
   59:     };
   60:   }
   61: 
   62:   if (impact.impactStatus === "awaiting_new_evidence") {
   63:     return {
   64:       phase: "awaiting_evidence",
   65:       headline: "The intervention needs a reassessment",
   66:       explanation:
   67:         "The baseline exists, but there is no new graded evidence yet. Assign a focused reassessment before deciding whether the intervention worked.",
   68:       evidenceReady: false,
   69:       reassessmentRecommended: true,
   70:       canCloseSuccessful: false,
   71:       recommendedDecision: "continue",
   72:       successSignals,
   73:       concernSignals,
   74:     };
   75:   }
   76: 
   77:   if (impact.impactStatus === "improving") {
   78:     return {
   79:       phase: "successful",
   80:       headline: "Evidence indicates that the intervention is working",
   81:       explanation:
   82:         "Multiple post-intervention indicators have improved. The teacher can continue briefly for consolidation or close the intervention as successful.",
   83:       evidenceReady: true,
   84:       reassessmentRecommended: false,
   85:       canCloseSuccessful: true,
   86:       recommendedDecision: "close_successful",
   87:       successSignals,
   88:       concernSignals,
   89:     };
   90:   }
   91: 
   92:   if (impact.impactStatus === "declining") {
   93:     return {
   94:       phase: "escalation_recommended",
   95:       headline: "The current support is not producing the required improvement",
   96:       explanation:
   97:         "Multiple indicators have weakened since the baseline. Review the misconception, change the support strategy and use a focused reassessment.",
   98:       evidenceReady: true,
   99:       reassessmentRecommended: true,
  100:       canCloseSuccessful: false,
  101:       recommendedDecision: "escalate",
  102:       successSignals,
  103:       concernSignals,
  104:     };
  105:   }
  106: 
  107:   if (impact.impactStatus === "mixed") {
  108:     return {
  109:       phase: "needs_retargeting",
  110:       headline: "Some evidence improved, but the intervention needs retargeting",
  111:       explanation:
  112:         "The response is mixed. Keep the successful elements, retarget the weak area and collect another meaningful assessment.",
  113:       evidenceReady: true,
  114:       reassessmentRecommended: true,
  115:       canCloseSuccessful: false,
  116:       recommendedDecision: "retarget",
  117:       successSignals,
  118:       concernSignals,
  119:     };
  120:   }
  121: 
  122:   return {
  123:     phase: "ready_to_review",
  124:     headline: "New evidence is available for teacher review",
  125:     explanation:
  126:       "There is post-intervention evidence, but the overall change is not yet large enough to classify as improving or declining.",
  127:     evidenceReady: true,
  128:     reassessmentRecommended: true,
  129:     canCloseSuccessful: false,
  130:     recommendedDecision: "continue",
  131:     successSignals,
  132:     concernSignals,
  133:   };
  134: }
  135: 
  136: export function buildInterventionReassessmentHref({
  137:   impact,
  138: }: {
  139:   impact: InterventionImpact;
  140: }): string {
  141:   return (
  142:     `/teacher/assignment-wizard?source=intervention-review` +
  143:     `&studentId=${encodeURIComponent(impact.studentId)}` +
  144:     `&topic=${encodeURIComponent(impact.topic)}` +
  145:     `&interventionId=${encodeURIComponent(impact.interventionId)}`
  146:   );
  147: }
  148: 
  149: export async function saveInterventionReviewDecision({
  150:   interventionId,
  151:   teacherId,
  152:   decision,
  153:   note,
  154: }: {
  155:   interventionId: string;
  156:   teacherId: string;
  157:   decision: InterventionReviewDecision;
  158:   note: string;
  159: }): Promise<void> {
  160:   const reference = doc(
  161:     db,
  162:     "interventions",
  163:     interventionId,
  164:   );
  165: 
  166:   const update: Record<string, unknown> = {
  167:     reviewDecision: decision,
  168:     reviewNote: note.trim(),
  169:     reviewedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  170:     reviewedBy: teacherId.trim(),
  171:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
  172:   };
  173: 
  174:   if (decision === "close_successful") {
  175:     update.status = "completed";
  176:     update.completedAt = serverTimestamp();
  177:   }
  178: 
  179:   if (decision === "escalate") {
  180:     update.status = "active";
  181:     update.escalatedAt = serverTimestamp();
  182:   }
  183: 
  184:   if (decision === "retarget") {
  185:     update.status = "active";
  186:     update.retargetedAt = serverTimestamp();
  187:   }
  188: 
  189:   await updateDoc(reference, update);
  190: }
