FILE: services\analytics\masteryAnalyticsService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import {
    2:   TOPIC_DEVELOPING_THRESHOLD,
    3:   TOPIC_SECURE_THRESHOLD,
    4: } from "@/data/analytics/analyticsConfig";
    5: import { getRecencyMultiplier } from "@/data/analytics/analyticsConfig";
    6: import type {
    7:   AnalyticsEvidence,
    8:   TopicMastery,
    9: } from "@/types/analytics";
   10: 
   11: function normaliseTopic(topic: string): string {
   12:   const cleaned = topic.trim();
   13: 
   14:   return cleaned || "General Computer Science";
   15: }
   16: 
   17: export function buildTopicMastery(
   18:   evidence: AnalyticsEvidence[],
   19: ): TopicMastery[] {
   20:   const graded = evidence.filter(
   21:     (item) =>
   22:       item.graded &&
   23:       typeof item.percentage === "number",
   24:   );
   25: 
   26:   const byTopic = new Map<
   27:     string,
   28:     AnalyticsEvidence[]
   29:   >();
   30: 
   31:   for (const item of graded) {
   32:     const topic = normaliseTopic(item.topic);
   33:     const current = byTopic.get(topic) || [];
   34: 
   35:     current.push(item);
   36:     byTopic.set(topic, current);
   37:   }
   38: 
   39:   const rows: TopicMastery[] = [];
   40: 
   41:   for (const [topic, items] of byTopic) {
   42:     let weightedTotal = 0;
   43:     let totalWeight = 0;
   44: 
   45:     for (const item of items) {
   46:       const weight =
   47:         item.weight *
   48:         getRecencyMultiplier(item.completedAt);
   49: 
   50:       weightedTotal +=
   51:         (item.percentage as number) * weight;
   52: 
   53:       totalWeight += weight;
   54:     }
   55: 
   56:     const weightedPercentage =
   57:       totalWeight > 0
   58:         ? Math.round(weightedTotal / totalWeight)
   59:         : 0;
   60: 
   61:     const recent = [...items].sort(
   62:       (a, b) =>
   63:         (b.completedAt?.getTime() ?? 0) -
   64:         (a.completedAt?.getTime() ?? 0),
   65:     )[0];
   66: 
   67:     const recentPercentage =
   68:       recent?.percentage ?? weightedPercentage;
   69: 
   70:     const status =
   71:       weightedPercentage >= TOPIC_SECURE_THRESHOLD
   72:         ? "secure"
   73:         : weightedPercentage >=
   74:             TOPIC_DEVELOPING_THRESHOLD
   75:           ? "developing"
   76:           : "priority";
   77: 
   78:     rows.push({
   79:       topic,
   80:       evidenceCount: items.length,
   81:       weightedPercentage,
   82:       recentPercentage,
   83:       status,
   84:     });
   85:   }
   86: 
   87:   return rows.sort(
   88:     (a, b) =>
   89:       b.weightedPercentage - a.weightedPercentage,
   90:   );
   91: }

FILE: services\examTrainerAnalyticsService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { getExamTrainerHistory } from "@/services/examTrainerAttemptService";
    2: import type {
    3:   ExamTrainerDashboardSummary,
    4:   ExamTrainerHistoryItem,
    5: } from "@/types/examTrainer";
    6: 
    7: function average(values: number[]): number {
    8:   if (values.length === 0) return 0;
    9:   return Math.round(
   10:     values.reduce((total, value) => total + value, 0) / values.length,
   11:   );
   12: }
   13: 
   14: function gradeFromPercentage(value: number): string {
   15:   if (value >= 90) return "9";
   16:   if (value >= 80) return "8";
   17:   if (value >= 70) return "7";
   18:   if (value >= 60) return "6";
   19:   if (value >= 50) return "5";
   20:   if (value >= 40) return "4";
   21:   if (value >= 30) return "3";
   22:   if (value >= 20) return "2";
   23:   return "1";
   24: }
   25: 
   26: function topicFrequency(
   27:   history: ExamTrainerHistoryItem[],
   28:   key: "priorityTopics",
   29: ): Map<string, number> {
   30:   const counts = new Map<string, number>();
   31: 
   32:   history.forEach((attempt) => {
   33:     attempt[key].forEach((topic) => {
   34:       counts.set(topic, (counts.get(topic) ?? 0) + 1);
   35:     });
   36:   });
   37: 
   38:   return counts;
   39: }
   40: 
   41: export async function getExamTrainerDashboardSummary(
   42:   studentId: string,
   43: ): Promise<ExamTrainerDashboardSummary> {
   44:   const history = await getExamTrainerHistory(studentId);
   45:   const percentages = history.map((attempt) => attempt.percentage);
   46:   const recent = percentages.slice(0, 3);
   47:   const previous = percentages.slice(3, 6);
   48: 
   49:   const weakestCounts = topicFrequency(history, "priorityTopics");
   50:   const weakestTopic =
   51:     [...weakestCounts.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0] ?? null;
   52: 
   53:   const topicPerformance = new Map<string, number[]>();
   54: 
   55:   history.forEach((attempt) => {
   56:     if (
   57:       attempt.selectedTopic !== "all" &&
   58:       !attempt.priorityTopics.includes(attempt.selectedTopic)
   59:     ) {
   60:       topicPerformance.set(attempt.selectedTopic, [
   61:         ...(topicPerformance.get(attempt.selectedTopic) ?? []),
   62:         attempt.percentage,
   63:       ]);
   64:     }
   65:   });
   66: 
   67:   const strongestTopic =
   68:     [...topicPerformance.entries()]
   69:       .map(([topic, scores]) => ({
   70:         topic,
   71:         average: average(scores),
   72:       }))
   73:       .sort((a, b) => b.average - a.average)[0]?.topic ?? null;
   74: 
   75:   const bestPercentage = percentages.length ? Math.max(...percentages) : 0;
   76: 
   77:   return {
   78:     completedAttempts: history.length,
   79:     averagePercentage: average(percentages),
   80:     bestPercentage,
   81:     bestGrade: gradeFromPercentage(bestPercentage),
   82:     latestPercentage: percentages[0] ?? 0,
   83:     weakestTopic,
   84:     strongestTopic,
   85:     recentTrend: average(recent) - average(previous),
   86:   };
   87: }

FILE: services\examTrainerEvidenceService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import {
    2:   collection,
    3:   getDocs,
    4:   orderBy,
    5:   query,
    6:   Timestamp,
    7: } from "firebase/firestore";
    8: 
    9: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   10: import type { AdaptiveEvidence } from "@/types/adaptiveLearning";
   11: 
   12: type ExamTrainerResultDocument = {
   13:   attemptId?: string;
   14:   topic?: string;
   15:   scorePercent?: number;
   16:   percentage?: number;
   17:   topicScores?: {
   18:     topic?: string;
   19:     percentage?: number;
   20:   }[];
   21:   completedAt?: Timestamp | null;
   22:   createdAt?: Timestamp | null;
   23: };
   24: 
   25: export async function getExamTrainerAdaptiveEvidence(
   26:   studentId: string,
   27: ): Promise<AdaptiveEvidence[]> {
   28:   const snapshot = await getDocs(
   29:     query(
   30:       collection(db, "users", studentId, "examTrainerResults"),
   31:       orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
   32:     ),
   33:   );
   34: 
   35:   const evidence: AdaptiveEvidence[] = [];
   36: 
   37:   snapshot.docs.forEach((document) => {
   38:     const data = document.data() as ExamTrainerResultDocument;
   39:     const completedAt =
   40:       data.completedAt instanceof Timestamp
   41:         ? data.completedAt.toDate()
   42:         : data.createdAt instanceof Timestamp
   43:           ? data.createdAt.toDate()
   44:           : null;
   45: 
   46:     if (Array.isArray(data.topicScores) && data.topicScores.length > 0) {
   47:       data.topicScores.forEach((topicScore, index) => {
   48:         if (!topicScore.topic) return;
   49: 
   50:         evidence.push({
   51:           id: `exam-trainer-${document.id}-${index}`,
   52:           topic: topicScore.topic,
   53:           source: "exam",
   54:           score:
   55:             typeof topicScore.percentage === "number"
   56:               ? topicScore.percentage
   57:               : 0,
   58:           completedAt,
   59:           weight: 1.25,
   60:         });
   61:       });
   62: 
   63:       return;
   64:     }
   65: 
   66:     evidence.push({
   67:       id: `exam-trainer-${document.id}`,
   68:       topic: data.topic || "Mixed Topics",
   69:       source: "exam",
   70:       score:
   71:         typeof data.scorePercent === "number"
   72:           ? data.scorePercent
   73:           : typeof data.percentage === "number"
   74:             ? data.percentage
   75:             : 0,
   76:       completedAt,
   77:       weight: 1.25,
   78:     });
   79:   });
   80: 
   81:   return evidence;
   82: }

FILE: services\examTrainerService.ts
==============================================================================================================
    1: import { indicativeGradeFromPercentage } from "@/services/qualificationGradeService";
    2: import type { Qualification } from "@/types/user";
    3: 
    4: import type {
    5:   ExamTrainerAnswer,
    6:   ExamTrainerDifficulty,
    7:   ExamTrainerMarkedAnswer,
    8:   ExamTrainerQuestion,
    9:   ExamTrainerReport,
   10: } from "@/types/examTrainer";
   11: 
   12: function normalise(value: string): string {
   13:   return value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
   14: }
   15: 
   16: function automaticMark(
   17:   question: ExamTrainerQuestion,
   18:   response: string,
   19: ): ExamTrainerMarkedAnswer | null {
   20:   const accepted = [
   21:     question.correctAnswer,
   22:     ...(question.acceptedAnswers ?? []),
   23:   ].filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value));
   24: 
   25:   if (question.type !== "multiple-choice" && accepted.length === 0) {
   26:     return null;
   27:   }
   28: 
   29:   const correct = accepted.some(
   30:     (answer) => normalise(answer) === normalise(response),
   31:   );
   32: 
   33:   return {
   34:     questionId: question.id,
   35:     awardedMarks: correct ? question.marks : 0,
   36:     maximumMarks: question.marks,
   37:     percentage: correct ? 100 : 0,
   38:     feedback: correct
   39:       ? "Correct. You have matched the expected answer."
   40:       : "This answer does not match the expected response.",
   41:     matchedPoints: correct ? question.markScheme : [],
   42:     missingPoints: correct ? [] : question.markScheme,
   43:     improvedAnswer: question.modelAnswer,
   44:     mode: "automatic",
   45:   };
   46: }
   47: 
   48: async function markWrittenAnswer(
   49:   question: ExamTrainerQuestion,
   50:   response: string,
   51: ): Promise<ExamTrainerMarkedAnswer> {
   52:   const apiResponse = await fetch("/api/ai/mark-lesson-question", {
   53:     method: "POST",
   54:     headers: {
   55:       "Content-Type": "application/json",
   56:     },
   57:     body: JSON.stringify({
   58:       topic: question.topic,
   59:       lessonTitle: "Exam Question Trainer",
   60:       question: question.question,
   61:       maximumMarks: question.marks,
   62:       modelAnswer: question.modelAnswer,
   63:       markScheme: question.markScheme,
   64:       guidance: question.examinerGuidance ?? [],
   65:       studentResponse: response,
   66:     }),
   67:   });
   68: 
   69:   const result = (await apiResponse.json()) as {
   70:     awardedMarks?: number;
   71:     maximumMarks?: number;
   72:     percentage?: number;
   73:     feedback?: string;
   74:     matchedPoints?: string[];
   75:     missingPoints?: string[];
   76:     improvedAnswer?: string;
   77:     mode?: "ai" | "demo";
   78:     error?: string;
   79:   };
   80: 
   81:   if (!apiResponse.ok || typeof result.awardedMarks !== "number") {
   82:     throw new Error(result.error || "The written answer could not be marked.");
   83:   }
   84: 
   85:   return {
   86:     questionId: question.id,
   87:     awardedMarks: result.awardedMarks,
   88:     maximumMarks: result.maximumMarks ?? question.marks,
   89:     percentage:
   90:       result.percentage ??
   91:       Math.round((result.awardedMarks / question.marks) * 100),
   92:     feedback: result.feedback ?? "Your answer has been marked.",
   93:     matchedPoints: result.matchedPoints ?? [],
   94:     missingPoints: result.missingPoints ?? [],
   95:     improvedAnswer: result.improvedAnswer ?? question.modelAnswer,
   96:     mode: result.mode ?? "demo",
   97:   };
   98: }
   99: 
  100: export function buildExamQuestions({
  101:   topic,
  102:   difficulty,
  103:   count,
  104:   questionBank,
  105: }: {
  106:   topic: string;
  107:   difficulty: ExamTrainerDifficulty | "all";
  108:   count: number;
  109:   questionBank: ExamTrainerQuestion[];
  110: }): ExamTrainerQuestion[] {
  111:   const filtered = questionBank.filter((question) => {
  112:     const topicMatches = topic === "all" || question.topic === topic;
  113:     const difficultyMatches =
  114:       difficulty === "all" || question.difficulty === difficulty;
  115: 
  116:     return topicMatches && difficultyMatches;
  117:   });
  118: 
  119:   const shuffled = [...filtered].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
  120:   return shuffled.slice(0, Math.max(1, Math.min(count, shuffled.length)));
  121: }
  122: 
  123: export async function markExamTrainerAttempt({
  124:   questions,
  125:   answers,
  126:   qualification,
  127: }: {
  128:   questions: ExamTrainerQuestion[];
  129:   answers: ExamTrainerAnswer[];
  130:   qualification?: Qualification | "";
  131: }): Promise<ExamTrainerReport> {
  132:   const markedAnswers = await Promise.all(
  133:     questions.map(async (question) => {
  134:       const response =
  135:         answers.find((answer) => answer.questionId === question.id)?.response ??
  136:         "";
  137: 
  138:       if (!response.trim()) {
  139:         return {
  140:           questionId: question.id,
  141:           awardedMarks: 0,
  142:           maximumMarks: question.marks,
  143:           percentage: 0,
  144:           feedback: "No answer was provided.",
  145:           matchedPoints: [],
  146:           missingPoints: question.markScheme,
  147:           improvedAnswer: question.modelAnswer,
  148:           mode: "automatic" as const,
  149:         };
  150:       }
  151: 
  152:       const automatic = automaticMark(question, response);
  153: 
  154:       if (automatic) {
  155:         return automatic;
  156:       }
  157: 
  158:       try {
  159:         return await markWrittenAnswer(question, response);
  160:       } catch {
  161:         return {
  162:           questionId: question.id,
  163:           awardedMarks: 0,
  164:           maximumMarks: question.marks,
  165:           percentage: 0,
  166:           feedback:
  167:             "Automatic marking was unavailable. Review the model answer and mark scheme.",
  168:           matchedPoints: [],
  169:           missingPoints: question.markScheme,
  170:           improvedAnswer: question.modelAnswer,
  171:           mode: "demo" as const,
  172:         };
  173:       }
  174:     }),
  175:   );
  176: 
  177:   const totalAwardedMarks = markedAnswers.reduce(
  178:     (total, answer) => total + answer.awardedMarks,
  179:     0,
  180:   );
  181: 
  182:   const totalAvailableMarks = questions.reduce(
  183:     (total, question) => total + question.marks,
  184:     0,
  185:   );
  186: 
  187:   const percentage =
  188:     totalAvailableMarks > 0
  189:       ? Math.round((totalAwardedMarks / totalAvailableMarks) * 100)
  190:       : 0;
  191: 
  192:   const topicMap = new Map<
  193:     string,
  194:     { awardedMarks: number; availableMarks: number }
  195:   >();
  196: 
  197:   questions.forEach((question) => {
  198:     const marked = markedAnswers.find(
  199:       (answer) => answer.questionId === question.id,
  200:     );
  201: 
  202:     const existing = topicMap.get(question.topic) ?? {
  203:       awardedMarks: 0,
  204:       availableMarks: 0,
  205:     };
  206: 
  207:     topicMap.set(question.topic, {
  208:       awardedMarks: existing.awardedMarks + (marked?.awardedMarks ?? 0),
  209:       availableMarks: existing.availableMarks + question.marks,
  210:     });
  211:   });
  212: 
  213:   const topicScores = Array.from(topicMap.entries())
  214:     .map(([topic, values]) => ({
  215:       topic,
  216:       awardedMarks: values.awardedMarks,
  217:       availableMarks: values.availableMarks,
  218:       percentage:
  219:         values.availableMarks > 0
  220:           ? Math.round((values.awardedMarks / values.availableMarks) * 100)
  221:           : 0,
  222:     }))
  223:     .sort((first, second) => second.percentage - first.percentage);
  224: 
  225: 
  226:   return {
  227:     totalAwardedMarks,
  228:     totalAvailableMarks,
  229:     percentage,
  230:     grade: indicativeGradeFromPercentage(percentage, qualification),
  231:     topicScores,
  232:     strongestTopics: topicScores
  233:       .filter((topic) => topic.percentage >= 70)
  234:       .map((topic) => topic.topic),
  235:     priorityTopics: topicScores
  236:       .filter((topic) => topic.percentage < 60)
  237:       .map((topic) => topic.topic),
  238:     markedAnswers,
  239:   };
  240: }

FILE: services\studentAdaptiveAnalyticsService.ts

    1: import {
    2:   collection,
    3:   getDocs,
    4:   orderBy,

    7:   where,

   10: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";

   16: export type AdaptiveTopicInsight = {
   17:   id: string;
   18:   topic: string;
   19:   averageScore: number;
   20:   attempts: number;
   21:   classification: "secure" | "developing" | "priority";
   22: };
   23: 
   24: export type AdaptiveRecommendation = {
   25:   id: string;
   26:   title: string;
   27:   description: string;
   28:   topic: string;
   29:   type: "lesson" | "quiz" | "exam";
   30:   href: string;
   31:   priority: "high" | "medium" | "positive";
   32: };

   34: export type StudentAdaptiveAnalytics = {
   35:   quizAverage: number;
   36:   examAverage: number;
   37:   combinedAverage: number;
   38:   currentGrade: string;
   39:   predictedGrade: string;
   40:   improvementTrend: number;
   41:   completedAssessments: number;
   42:   awaitingMarking: number;
   43:   strongestTopics: AdaptiveTopicInsight[];
   44:   priorityTopics: AdaptiveTopicInsight[];
   45:   recommendations: AdaptiveRecommendation[];

   48: export const emptyStudentAdaptiveAnalytics: StudentAdaptiveAnalytics = {
   49:   quizAverage: 0,
   50:   examAverage: 0,
   51:   combinedAverage: 0,
   52:   currentGrade: "1",
   53:   predictedGrade: "1",
   54:   improvementTrend: 0,
   55:   completedAssessments: 0,
   56:   awaitingMarking: 0,
   57:   strongestTopics: [],
   58:   priorityTopics: [],
   59:   recommendations: [],
   60: };
   61: 
   62: function safeNumber(value: unknown): number {
   63:   return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;
   64: }
   65: 
   66: function safeString(value: unknown, fallback = ""): string {
   67:   return typeof value === "string" && value.trim() ? value.trim() : fallback;
   68: }
   69: 
   70: function average(values: number[]): number {
   71:   if (!values.length) {
   72:     return 0;
   73:   }
   74: 
   75:   return Math.round(
   76:     values.reduce((total, value) => total + value, 0) / values.length,
   77:   );
   78: }
   79: 
   80: export async function getStudentAdaptiveAnalytics(
   81:   studentId: string,
   82: ): Promise<StudentAdaptiveAnalytics> {
   83:   const cleanedStudentId = studentId.trim();
   84: 
   85:   if (!cleanedStudentId) {
   86:     return emptyStudentAdaptiveAnalytics;
   87:   }
   88: 
   89:   const [
   90:     profile,
   91:     quizSnapshot,
   92:     assignedQuizResultsSnapshot,
   93:     examAssignments,
   94:   ] = await Promise.all([
   95:       getUserProfile(cleanedStudentId),
   96:       getDocs(
   97:         query(
   98:           collection(db, "users", cleanedStudentId, "quizResults"),
   99:           orderBy("createdAt", "desc"),
  100:         ),
  101:       ),
  102: 
  103:       getDocs(
  104:         query(
  105:           collection(db, "assignmentResults"),
  106:           where("studentId", "==", cleanedStudentId),
  107:         ),
  108:       ),
  109: 
  110:       getStudentExamAssignments(cleanedStudentId),
  111:     ]);
  112: 
  113:   const quizResults = quizSnapshot.docs.map((document) => {
  114:     const data = document.data();
  115: 
  116:     const createdAt =
  117:       data.createdAt instanceof Timestamp ? data.createdAt.toDate() : null;
  118: 
  119:     return {
  120:       id: document.id,
  121:       topic:
  122:         safeString(data.topic) ||
  123:         safeString(data.topicTitle) ||
  124:         safeString(data.title, "Quiz"),
  125:       percentage: safeNumber(data.scorePercent),
  126:       completedAt: createdAt,
  127:     };
  128:   });
  129: 
  130:   const assignedQuizResults = assignedQuizResultsSnapshot.docs
  131:     .map((document) => {
  132:       const data = document.data();
  133: 
  134:       return {
  135:         id: document.id,
  136:         topic:
  137:           safeString(data.topic) ||
  138:           safeString(data.topicTitle) ||
  139:           safeString(data.quizTitle) ||
  140:           safeString(data.title, "Assigned quiz"),
  141:         percentage: safeNumber(data.percentage),
  142:         completedAt:
  143:           data.completedAt instanceof Timestamp
  144:             ? data.completedAt.toDate()
  145:             : null,
  146:       };
  147:     })
  148:     .filter((result) => result.percentage >= 0);
  149: 
  150:   const submissions = await Promise.all(
  151:     examAssignments.map((assignment) =>
  152:       getExamSubmission(assignment.id, cleanedStudentId),
  153:     ),
  154:   );
  155: 
  156:   const examResults = examAssignments
  157:     .map((assignment, index) => {
  158:       const submission = submissions[index];
  159: 
  160:       if (!submission || submission.status !== "marked") {
  161:         return null;
  162:       }
  163: 
  164:       return {
  165:         id: assignment.id,
  166:         topic:
  167:           assignment.questionSetSnapshot.topic ||
  168:           assignment.questionSetTitle ||
  169:           assignment.title,
  170:         percentage: submission.percentage,
  171:         completedAt: submission.markedAt,
  172:       };
  173:     })
  174:     .filter((result): result is NonNullable<typeof result> => result !== null);
  175: 
  176:   const awaitingMarking = submissions.filter(
  177:     (submission) =>
  178:       submission?.status === "submitted" || submission?.status === "marking",
  179:   ).length;
  180: 
  181:   const allQuizResults = [...quizResults, ...assignedQuizResults];
  182: 
  183:   const quizScores = allQuizResults.map((result) => result.percentage);
  184: 
  185:   const examScores = examResults.map((result) => result.percentage);
  186: 
  187:   const combinedResults = [
  188:     ...allQuizResults.map((result) => ({
  189:       ...result,
  190:       source: "quiz" as const,
  191:     })),
  192:     ...examResults.map((result) => ({
  193:       ...result,
  194:       source: "exam" as const,
  195:     })),
  196:   ];
  197: 
  198:   const topicGroups = new Map<string, number[]>();
  199: 
  200:   combinedResults.forEach((result) => {
  201:     const scores = topicGroups.get(result.topic) || [];
  202: 
  203:     scores.push(result.percentage);
  204: 
  205:     topicGroups.set(result.topic, scores);
  206:   });
  207: 
  208:   const topics: AdaptiveTopicInsight[] = Array.from(topicGroups.entries())
  209:     .map(([topic, scores], index) => {
  210:       const averageScore = average(scores);
  211: 
  212:       return {
  213:         id: `adaptive-topic-${index}`,
  214:         topic,
  215:         averageScore,
  216:         attempts: scores.length,
  217:         classification:
  218:           averageScore >= 70
  219:             ? ("secure" as const)
  220:             : averageScore < 50
  221:               ? ("priority" as const)
  222:               : ("developing" as const),
  223:       };
  224:     })
  225:     .sort((first, second) => second.averageScore - first.averageScore);
  226: 
  227:   const strongestTopics = topics
  228:     .filter((topic) => topic.classification === "secure")
  229:     .slice(0, 3);
  230: 
  231:   const priorityTopics = [...topics]
  232:     .filter((topic) => topic.classification === "priority")
  233:     .sort((first, second) => first.averageScore - second.averageScore)
  234:     .slice(0, 3);
  235: 
  236:   const chronological = combinedResults
  237:     .filter((result) => result.completedAt)
  238:     .sort(
  239:       (first, second) =>
  240:         (first.completedAt?.getTime() || 0) -
  241:         (second.completedAt?.getTime() || 0),
  242:     )
  243:     .map((result) => result.percentage);
  244: 
  245:   let improvementTrend = 0;
  246: 
  247:   if (chronological.length >= 2) {
  248:     const midpoint = Math.ceil(chronological.length / 2);
  249: 
  250:     improvementTrend =
  251:       average(chronological.slice(midpoint)) -
  252:       average(chronological.slice(0, midpoint));
  253:   }
  254: 
  255:   const quizAverage = average(quizScores);
  256: 
  257:   const examAverage = average(examScores);
  258: 
  259:   const combinedAverage = average([...quizScores, ...examScores]);
  260: 
  261:   const recommendations: AdaptiveRecommendation[] = [];
  262: 
  263:   const priorityTopic = priorityTopics[0];
  264: 
  265:   if (priorityTopic) {
  266:     const encodedTopic = encodeURIComponent(priorityTopic.topic);
  267: 
  268:     recommendations.push(
  269:       {
  270:         id: "priority-lesson",
  271:         title: `Review ${priorityTopic.topic}`,
  272:         description: `Current assessed performance is ${priorityTopic.averageScore}%. Revisit the core lesson before practising again.`,
  273:         topic: priorityTopic.topic,
  274:         type: "lesson",
  275:         href: `/learn?search=${encodedTopic}`,
  276:         priority: "high",
  277:       },
  278:       {
  279:         id: "priority-quiz",
  280:         title: "Complete retrieval practice",
  281:         description: `Use a short quiz on ${priorityTopic.topic} to check misconceptions immediately.`,
  282:         topic: priorityTopic.topic,
  283:         type: "quiz",
  284:         href: `/quiz?topic=${encodedTopic}`,
  285:         priority: "high",
  286:       },
  287:       {
  288:         id: "priority-exam",
  289:         title: "Attempt an exam-style question",
  290:         description: `Apply your knowledge of ${priorityTopic.topic} in a written response and compare it with the mark scheme.`,
  291:         topic: priorityTopic.topic,
  292:         type: "exam",
  293:         href: "/assignments?filter=exams",
  294:         priority: "medium",
  295:       },
  296:     );
  297:   } else {
  298:     recommendations.push({
  299:       id: "maintain",
  300:       title: "Maintain your progress",
  301:       description:
  302:         "Complete a mixed retrieval quiz and one written exam question to keep secure topics active.",
  303:       topic: strongestTopics[0]?.topic || "Computer Science",
  304:       type: "quiz",
  305:       href: "/quiz",
  306:       priority: "positive",
  307:     });
  308:   }
  309: 
  310:   return {
  311:     quizAverage,
  312:     examAverage,
  313:     combinedAverage,
  314:     currentGrade: indicativeGradeFromPercentage(
  315:       combinedAverage,
  316:       profile?.qualification,
  317:     ),
  318:     predictedGrade: indicativeGradeFromPercentage(
  319:       Math.max(
  320:         0,
  321:         Math.min(100, combinedAverage + Math.round(improvementTrend * 0.4)),
  322:       ),
  323:       profile?.qualification,
  324:     ),
  325:     improvementTrend,
  326:     completedAssessments: combinedResults.length,
  327:     awaitingMarking,
  328:     strongestTopics,
  329:     priorityTopics,
  330:     recommendations,
  331:   };
  332: }

   25:     predictedGrade: analytics.predictedGrade,
   26:     improvementTrend: analytics.improvementTrend,
   27:     completedLessons: profile.completedLessons.length,
   28:     completedAssessments: analytics.completedAssessments,
   29:     awaitingMarking: analytics.awaitingMarking,

   32:       averageScore: t.averageScore,
   33:       attempts: t.attempts,
   34:     })),
   35:     priorityTopics: analytics.priorityTopics.map((t) => ({
   36:       topic: t.topic,
   37:       averageScore: t.averageScore,
   38:       attempts: t.attempts,
   39:     })),
   40:     recommendedActions: analytics.recommendations.map((r) => ({

   42:       description: r.description,

    1: import {
    2:   addDoc,
    3:   collection,
    4:   doc,
    5:   getDoc,
    6:   serverTimestamp,
    7:   Timestamp,
    8:   updateDoc,
    9: } from "firebase/firestore";
   10: import { db } from "@/lib/firebase";
   11: import type {
   12:   TutorConversation,
   13:   TutorMessage,
   14:   TutorResponse,
   15:   TutorStudentContext,
   16: } from "@/types/studentTutor";
   17: 
   18: type FMsg = Omit<TutorMessage, "createdAt"> & { createdAt?: Timestamp };
   19: type FConv = {
   20:   studentId: string;
   21:   title: string;
   22:   messages?: FMsg[];
   23:   createdAt?: Timestamp;
   24:   updatedAt?: Timestamp;
   25: };
   26: const toDate = (v?: Timestamp | null) => (v?.toDate ? v.toDate() : null);
   27: const convert = (id: string, d: FConv): TutorConversation => ({
   28:   id,
   29:   studentId: d.studentId,
   30:   title: d.title || "Tutor conversation",
   31:   messages: (d.messages || []).map((m) => ({
   32:     ...m,
   33:     createdAt: toDate(m.createdAt),
   34:   })),
   35:   createdAt: toDate(d.createdAt),
   36:   updatedAt: toDate(d.updatedAt),
   37: });
   38: const mid = () =>
   39:   `message-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`;
   40: export async function createTutorConversation(studentId: string) {
   41:   const r = await addDoc(collection(db, "studentTutorConversations"), {
   42:     studentId: studentId.trim(),
   43:     title: "New tutor conversation",
   44:     messages: [],
   45:     createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
   46:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   47:   });
   48:   return r.id;
   49: }
   50: export async function getTutorConversation(id: string) {
   51:   const s = await getDoc(doc(db, "studentTutorConversations", id));
   52:   return s.exists() ? convert(s.id, s.data() as FConv) : null;
   53: }
   54: export async function saveTutorExchange(input: {
   55:   conversationId: string;
   56:   studentId: string;
   57:   studentMessage: string;
   58:   tutorResponse: TutorResponse;
   59: }) {
   60:   const c = await getTutorConversation(input.conversationId);
   61:   if (!c || c.studentId !== input.studentId)
   62:     throw new Error("Tutor conversation unavailable.");
   63:   const now = new Date();
   64:   const messages = [
   65:     ...c.messages,
   66:     {
   67:       id: mid(),
   68:       role: "student" as const,
   69:       content: input.studentMessage.trim(),
   70:       createdAt: now,
   71:     },
   72:     {
   73:       id: mid(),
   74:       role: "assistant" as const,
   75:       content: input.tutorResponse.reply,
   76:       createdAt: now,
   77:       mode: input.tutorResponse.mode,
   78:     },
   79:   ];
   80:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "studentTutorConversations", input.conversationId), {
   81:     title: c.messages.length
   82:       ? c.title
   83:       : input.studentMessage.trim().slice(0, 60),
   84:     messages: messages.map((m) => ({
   85:       ...m,
   86:       createdAt: Timestamp.fromDate(m.createdAt || now),
   87:     })),
   88:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   89:   });
   90: }
   91: export async function clearTutorConversation(id: string, studentId: string) {
   92:   const c = await getTutorConversation(id);
   93:   if (!c || c.studentId !== studentId)
   94:     throw new Error("Tutor conversation unavailable.");
   95:   await updateDoc(doc(db, "studentTutorConversations", id), {
   96:     title: "New tutor conversation",
   97:     messages: [],
   98:     updatedAt: serverTimestamp(),
   99:   });
  100: }
  101: export async function requestTutorResponse(input: {
  102:   studentId: string;
  103:   conversationId: string;
  104:   message: string;
  105:   history: { role: "student" | "assistant"; content: string }[];
  106:   context: TutorStudentContext;
  107: }): Promise<TutorResponse> {
  108:   const r = await fetch("/api/ai/student-tutor", {
  109:     method: "POST",
  110:     headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
  111:     body: JSON.stringify(input),
  112:   });
  113:   const j = await r.json();
  114:   if (!r.ok) throw new Error(j.error || "The tutor could not respond.");
  115:   return j as TutorResponse;
  116: }
